0029:

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/cli.py
0001: import argparse
0002: import sys
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize, LexicalError
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser, SyntaxError_
0005: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze, SemanticError
0006: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import lower_to_ir
0007: from ds_pipeline_dsl.optimizer import optimize
0008: from ds_pipeline_dsl.executor import execute
0009: 
0010: def main():
0011:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="DS Pipeline DSL Compiler")
0012:     parser.add_argument("file", help="Path to the .pipe source file")
0013:     parser.add_argument("--tokens", action="store_true", help="Print lexer token output")
0014:     parser.add_argument("--ast", action="store_true", help="Print parsed AST")
0015:     parser.add_argument("--ir", action="store_true", help="Print unoptimized IR")
0016:     parser.add_argument("--optimized-ir", action="store_true", help="Print optimized IR")
0017:     parser.add_argument("--trace", action="store_true", help="Print executor trace")
0018:     
0019:     args = parser.parse_args()
0020:     
0021:     # If no flags provided, set all to True
0022:     if not (args.tokens or args.ast or args.ir or getattr(args, 'optimized_ir') or args.trace):
0023:         args.tokens = True
0024:         args.ast = True
0025:         args.ir = True
0026:         args.optimized_ir = True
0027:         args.trace = True
0028:         
0029:     try:
0030:         with open(args.file, "r") as f:
0031:             code = f.read()
0032:     except Exception as e:
0033:         print(f"Error reading file: {e}")
0034:         sys.exit(1)
0035:         
0036:     try:
0037:         tokens = tokenize(code)
0038:         if args.tokens:
0039:             print("=== TOKENS ===")
0040:             for tok in tokens:
0041:                 print(tok)
0042:                 
0043:         ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0044:         if args.ast:
0045:             print("=== AST ===")
0046:             print(ast)
0047:             
0048:         table = analyze(ast)
0049:         
0050:         ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0051:         if args.ir:
0052:             print("=== IR ===")
0053:             for i in ir:
0054:                 print(i)
0055:                 
0056:         notes = []
0057:         opt_ir = optimize(ir, notes)
0058:         if args.optimized_ir:
0059:             print("=== OPTIMIZED IR ===")
0060:             for i in opt_ir:
0061:                 print(i)
0062:             print("=== OPTIMIZATION NOTES ===")
0063:             if notes:
0064:                 for note in notes:
0065:                     print(note)
0066:             else:
0067:                 print("(no optimizations applied)")
0068:             print(f"IR instructions: {len(ir)} -> {len(opt_ir)}")
0069:                 
0070:         trace = execute(opt_ir)
0071:         if args.trace:
0072:             print("=== TRACE ===")
0073:             for line in trace:
0074:                 print(line)
0075:                 
0076:     except LexicalError as e:
0077:         print(e)
0078:         sys.exit(1)
0079:     except SyntaxError_ as e:
0080:         print(e)
0081:         sys.exit(1)
0082:     except SemanticError as e:
0083:         print(e)
0084:         sys.exit(1)
0085: 
0086: if __name__ == "__main__":
0087:     main()

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/executor.py
0001: from typing import List
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import IRInstruction
0003: 
0004: def execute(ir_list: List[IRInstruction]) -> List[str]:
0005:     trace = []
0006:     for instr in ir_list:
0007:         if instr.op == "OP_LOAD":
0008:             trace.append(f"Loaded {instr.args['filename']}")
0009:         elif instr.op == "OP_CLEAN":
0010:             trace.append(f"Cleaned using {instr.args['strategy']}")
0011:         elif instr.op == "OP_SELECT":
0012:             cols = ", ".join(instr.args["columns"])
0013:             trace.append(f"Selected columns: {cols}")
0014:         elif instr.op == "OP_DROP":
0015:             cols = ", ".join(instr.args["columns"])
0016:             trace.append(f"Dropped columns: {cols}")
0017:         elif instr.op == "OP_SPLIT":
0018:             percent = instr.args["percent"]
0019:             if percent == int(percent):
0020:                 percent = int(percent)
0021:             trace.append(f"Defined split '{instr.args['name']}' = {percent}%")
0022:         elif instr.op == "OP_TRAIN":
0023:             trace.append(f"Trained model '{instr.args['model_name']}' using {instr.args['algorithm']}")
0024:         elif instr.op == "OP_EVAL":
0025:             trace.append(f"Evaluated model '{instr.args['model_name']}' (simulated accuracy trace)")
0026:         else:
0027:             trace.append(f"Unknown instruction: {instr.op}")
0028:     return trace

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/ir.py
0001: from dataclasses import dataclass
0002: from typing import List
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ast_nodes import (
0004:     PipelineNode, LoadStmt, CleanStmt, SelectStmt, DropStmt,
0005:     SplitStmt, TrainStmt, EvaluateStmt
0006: )
0007: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import SymbolTable
0008: 
0009: @dataclass
0010: class IRInstruction:
0011:     op: str
0012:     args: dict
0013: 
0014: def lower_to_ir(pipeline_ast: PipelineNode, symbol_table: SymbolTable) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0015:     ir = []
0016:     for stmt in pipeline_ast.statements:
0017:         if isinstance(stmt, LoadStmt):
0018:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_LOAD", args={"filename": stmt.filename, "line": stmt.line}))
0019:         elif isinstance(stmt, CleanStmt):
0020:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_CLEAN", args={"strategy": stmt.strategy, "line": stmt.line}))
0021:         elif isinstance(stmt, SelectStmt):
0022:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_SELECT", args={"columns": stmt.columns, "line": stmt.line}))
0023:         elif isinstance(stmt, DropStmt):
0024:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_DROP", args={"columns": stmt.columns, "line": stmt.line}))
0025:         elif isinstance(stmt, SplitStmt):
0026:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_SPLIT", args={"name": stmt.name, "percent": stmt.percent, "line": stmt.line}))
0027:         elif isinstance(stmt, TrainStmt):
0028:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_TRAIN", args={"model_name": stmt.model_name, "algorithm": stmt.algorithm, "line": stmt.line}))
0029:         elif isinstance(stmt, EvaluateStmt):
0030:             ir.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_EVAL", args={"model_name": stmt.model_name, "line": stmt.line}))
0031:     return ir

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/lexer.py
0001: import re
0002: from dataclasses import dataclass
0003: from typing import List
0004: 
0005: KEYWORDS = {
0006:     "pipeline", "load", "clean", "select", "split", "train", "evaluate", "drop"
0007: }
0008: 
0009: TOKEN_SPEC = [
0010:     ("STRING",     r'"[^"\n]*"'),
0011:     ("BAD_STRING", r'"[^"\n]*'),
0012:     ("PERCENT",    r'\d+(\.\d+)?%'),
0013:     ("NUMBER",     r'\d+(\.\d+)?'),
0014:     ("LBRACE",     r'\{'),
0015:     ("RBRACE",     r'\}'),
0016:     ("EQUALS",     r'='),
0017:     ("SEMI",       r';'),
0018:     ("COMMA",      r','),
0019:     ("IDENT",      r'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*'),
0020:     ("SKIP",       r'[ \t\r\n]+'),
0021:     ("COMMENT",    r'//[^\n]*'),
0022:     ("MISMATCH",   r'.'),
0023: ]
0024: 
0025: MASTER_PATTERN = re.compile(
0026:     "|".join(f"(?P<{name}>{pattern})" for name, pattern in TOKEN_SPEC)
0027: )
0028: 
0029: @dataclass
0030: class Token:
0031:     type: str
0032:     value: str
0033:     line: int
0034:     col: int
0035: 
0036: class LexicalError(Exception):
0037:     pass
0038: 
0039: def tokenize(source: str) -> List[Token]:
0040:     tokens = []
0041:     line_num = 1
0042:     line_start = 0
0043:     for match in MASTER_PATTERN.finditer(source):
0044:         kind = match.lastgroup
0045:         value = match.group()
0046:         col = match.start() - line_start
0047: 
0048:         if kind == "SKIP" or kind == "COMMENT":
0049:             line_num += value.count("\n")
0050:             if "\n" in value:
0051:                 line_start = match.end() - len(value.split("\n")[-1])
0052:             continue
0053:         if kind == "BAD_STRING":
0054:             raise LexicalError(
0055:                 f"Lexical Error at line {line_num}, col {col}: unterminated string literal"
0056:             )
0057:         if kind == "MISMATCH":
0058:             raise LexicalError(
0059:                 f"Lexical Error at line {line_num}, col {col}: "
0060:                 f"unexpected character {value!r}"
0061:             )
0062:         if kind == "IDENT" and value in KEYWORDS:
0063:             kind = value.upper()
0064: 
0065:         tokens.append(Token(kind, value, line_num, col))
0066: 
0067:     tokens.append(Token("EOF", "", line_num, 0))
0068:     return tokens

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/optimizer.py
0001: from typing import List, Optional
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import IRInstruction
0003: 
0004: def _note(notes: Optional[list], msg: str) -> None:
0005:     if notes is not None:
0006:         notes.append(msg)
0007: 
0008: def _remove_unreachable(ir_list: List[IRInstruction], notes: Optional[list] = None) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0009:     """Pass 1: Removes instructions appearing after the last OP_EVAL.
0010:     Safety: Pipeline records are produced only at OP_EVAL points, so any instructions
0011:     after the final evaluation cannot affect any observable evaluation records."""
0012:     last_eval_idx = None
0013:     for idx in range(len(ir_list) - 1, -1, -1):
0014:         if ir_list[idx].op == "OP_EVAL":
0015:             last_eval_idx = idx
0016:             break
0017: 
0018:     if last_eval_idx is None:
0019:         return [IRInstruction(i.op, dict(i.args)) for i in ir_list]
0020: 
0021:     for instr in ir_list[last_eval_idx + 1:]:
0022:         line = instr.args.get("line", "?") if instr.args else "?"
0023:         _note(notes, f"unreachable: removed {instr.op} (line {line}) after last OP_EVAL")
0024: 
0025:     return [IRInstruction(i.op, dict(i.args)) for i in ir_list[:last_eval_idx + 1]]
0026: 
0027: def _remove_dead_trains(ir_list: List[IRInstruction], notes: Optional[list] = None) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0028:     """Pass 2: Removes OP_TRAIN instructions whose models are never evaluated.
0029:     Policy: A pipeline with no evaluation is left untouched (this is a conservative policy,
0030:     not a correctness requirement).
0031:     Safety: OP_TRAIN writes only models[name], which is read only at OP_EVAL. Models that
0032:     are never evaluated have no observable effect."""
0033:     has_eval = any(i.op == "OP_EVAL" for i in ir_list)
0034:     if not has_eval:
0035:         # Conservative policy: a pipeline with no evaluation is left untouched
0036:         return [IRInstruction(i.op, dict(i.args)) for i in ir_list]
0037: 
0038:     # Robustness: if any OP_EVAL lacks model_name, treat all models as live (change nothing)
0039:     for instr in ir_list:
0040:         if instr.op == "OP_EVAL":
0041:             if not instr.args or "model_name" not in instr.args:
0042:                 return [IRInstruction(i.op, dict(i.args)) for i in ir_list]
0043: 
0044:     live = set()
0045:     kept_reversed = []
0046:     dead_notes_reversed = []
0047: 
0048:     for instr in reversed(ir_list):
0049:         if instr.op == "OP_EVAL":
0050:             mname = instr.args.get("model_name")
0051:             if mname is not None:
0052:                 live.add(mname)
0053:             kept_reversed.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, dict(instr.args)))
0054:         elif instr.op == "OP_TRAIN":
0055:             mname = instr.args.get("model_name") if instr.args else None
0056:             if mname is None or mname in live:
0057:                 if mname is not None:
0058:                     live.discard(mname)
0059:                 kept_reversed.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, dict(instr.args)))
0060:             else:
0061:                 line = instr.args.get("line", "?") if instr.args else "?"
0062:                 dead_notes_reversed.append(
0063:                     f"dead-train: removed OP_TRAIN '{mname}' (line {line}); model is never evaluated"
0064:                 )
0065:         else:
0066:             kept_reversed.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, dict(instr.args)))
0067: 
0068:     kept_reversed.reverse()
0069:     dead_notes_reversed.reverse()
0070:     for note_msg in dead_notes_reversed:
0071:         _note(notes, note_msg)
0072: 
0073:     return kept_reversed
0074: 
0075: def _merge_adjacent_drops(ir_list: List[IRInstruction], notes: Optional[list] = None) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0076:     """Pass 3: Merges immediately adjacent runs of >= 2 OP_DROP instructions.
0077:     Safety: Dropping columns is set union/difference with no observable state
0078:     between adjacent drop statements."""
0079:     result = []
0080:     i = 0
0081:     n = len(ir_list)
0082: 
0083:     while i < n:
0084:         instr = ir_list[i]
0085:         if instr.op == "OP_DROP" and instr.args and "columns" in instr.args and isinstance(instr.args["columns"], list):
0086:             run = [instr]
0087:             j = i + 1
0088:             while j < n and ir_list[j].op == "OP_DROP" and ir_list[j].args and "columns" in ir_list[j].args and isinstance(ir_list[j].args["columns"], list):
0089:                 run.append(ir_list[j])
0090:                 j += 1
0091: 
0092:             if len(run) >= 2:
0093:                 seen = set()
0094:                 merged_cols = []
0095:                 lines = []
0096:                 for d in run:
0097:                     line_val = d.args.get("line", "?")
0098:                     lines.append(str(line_val))
0099:                     for col in d.args.get("columns", []):
0100:                         if col not in seen:
0101:                             seen.add(col)
0102:                             merged_cols.append(col)
0103: 
0104:                 new_args = dict(run[0].args)
0105:                 new_args["columns"] = merged_cols
0106:                 result.append(IRInstruction(op="OP_DROP", args=new_args))
0107: 
0108:                 lines_str = ", ".join(lines)
0109:                 _note(notes, f"drop-merge: merged {len(run)} adjacent OP_DROP (lines {lines_str}) into one")
0110:                 i = j
0111:             else:
0112:                 result.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, dict(instr.args)))
0113:                 i += 1
0114:         else:
0115:             args = dict(instr.args) if instr.args else {}
0116:             result.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, args))
0117:             i += 1
0118: 
0119:     return result
0120: 
0121: def _args_match_excluding_line(args1: dict, args2: dict) -> bool:
0122:     k1 = {k: v for k, v in args1.items() if k != "line"}
0123:     k2 = {k: v for k, v in args2.items() if k != "line"}
0124:     return k1 == k2
0125: 
0126: def _collapse_duplicates(ir_list: List[IRInstruction], notes: Optional[list] = None) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0127:     """Pass 4: Collapses consecutive duplicate OP_CLEAN or OP_SELECT instructions.
0128:     Safety: An immediately repeated clean with identical strategy is idempotent under A1.
0129:     An immediately repeated select with identical columns is also idempotent."""
0130:     result = []
0131:     for instr in ir_list:
0132:         args = dict(instr.args) if instr.args else {}
0133:         if instr.op in ("OP_CLEAN", "OP_SELECT") and len(result) > 0:
0134:             last_kept = result[-1]
0135:             last_args = last_kept.args if last_kept.args else {}
0136:             if instr.op == last_kept.op and _args_match_excluding_line(args, last_args):
0137:                 line = args.get("line", "?")
0138:                 _note(notes, f"duplicate: removed repeated {instr.op} (line {line})")
0139:                 continue
0140:         result.append(IRInstruction(instr.op, args))
0141:     return result
0142: 
0143: def optimize(ir_list: List[IRInstruction], notes: Optional[list] = None) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0144:     """Optimizes the IR instructions through four independent passes:
0145:     1. _remove_unreachable: removes dead tail instructions after the final evaluation.
0146:     2. _remove_dead_trains: removes training of models that are never evaluated.
0147:     3. _merge_adjacent_drops: combines runs of adjacent drop instructions.
0148:     4. _collapse_duplicates: eliminates consecutive duplicate clean and select instructions.
0149: 
0150:     Note on order: Order of passes is chosen for readability; the passes are independent
0151:     because all data-preparation statements precede the first train (F1).
0152:     """
0153:     ir = _remove_unreachable(ir_list, notes)
0154:     ir = _remove_dead_trains(ir, notes)
0155:     ir = _merge_adjacent_drops(ir, notes)
0156:     ir = _collapse_duplicates(ir, notes)
0157:     return ir

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/parser.py
0001: r"""
0002: Complete Grammar Specification (EBNF):
0003: 
0004: pipeline_decl    ::= 'pipeline' IDENT '{' statement* '}'
0005: statement        ::= load_stmt
0006:                    | clean_stmt
0007:                    | select_stmt
0008:                    | drop_stmt
0009:                    | split_stmt
0010:                    | train_stmt
0011:                    | evaluate_stmt
0012: 
0013: load_stmt        ::= 'load' STRING ';'
0014: clean_stmt       ::= 'clean' IDENT ';'
0015: select_stmt      ::= 'select' IDENT (',' IDENT)* ';'
0016: drop_stmt        ::= 'drop' IDENT (',' IDENT)* ';'
0017: split_stmt       ::= 'split' split_name '=' PERCENT ';'
0018: split_name       ::= IDENT | 'train'
0019: train_stmt       ::= 'train' IDENT '=' IDENT ';'
0020: evaluate_stmt    ::= 'evaluate' IDENT ';'
0021: 
0022: Lexical Notes:
0023:   IDENT          ::= [A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*
0024:   PERCENT        ::= [0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?'%'
0025:   STRING         ::= '"' [^"\\n]* '"'
0026:   Comments       ::= '//' [^\\n]* (single-line comments to end of line)
0027:   Whitespace     ::= [ \t\r\n]+ (skipped)
0028: """
0029: 
0030: from typing import List
0031: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import Token
0032: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ast_nodes import (
0033:     PipelineNode, LoadStmt, CleanStmt, SelectStmt, DropStmt,
0034:     SplitStmt, TrainStmt, EvaluateStmt
0035: )
0036: 
0037: class SyntaxError_(Exception):
0038:     pass
0039: 
0040: class Parser:
0041:     def __init__(self, tokens: List[Token]):
0042:         self.tokens = tokens
0043:         self.pos = 0
0044: 
0045:     def peek(self) -> Token:
0046:         return self.tokens[self.pos]
0047: 
0048:     def advance(self) -> Token:
0049:         tok = self.tokens[self.pos]
0050:         self.pos += 1
0051:         return tok
0052: 
0053:     def expect(self, ttype: str) -> Token:
0054:         tok = self.peek()
0055:         if tok.type != ttype:
0056:             raise SyntaxError_(
0057:                 f"Syntax Error at line {tok.line}: expected {ttype}, "
0058:                 f"found {tok.type} ({tok.value!r})"
0059:             )
0060:         return self.advance()
0061: 
0062:     def parse_pipeline(self) -> PipelineNode:
0063:         self.expect("PIPELINE")
0064:         name_tok = self.expect("IDENT")
0065:         self.expect("LBRACE")
0066:         stmts = []
0067:         while self.peek().type != "RBRACE":
0068:             if self.peek().type == "EOF":
0069:                 tok = self.peek()
0070:                 raise SyntaxError_(
0071:                     f"Syntax Error at line {tok.line}: unexpected end of file; expected '}}' to close pipeline '{name_tok.value}'"
0072:                 )
0073:             stmts.append(self.parse_statement())
0074:         self.expect("RBRACE")
0075: 
0076:         tok = self.peek()
0077:         if tok.type == "PIPELINE":
0078:             raise SyntaxError_(
0079:                 f"Syntax Error at line {tok.line}: only one pipeline per file is supported"
0080:             )
0081:         if tok.type != "EOF":
0082:             raise SyntaxError_(
0083:                 f"Syntax Error at line {tok.line}: unexpected token {tok.type} ({tok.value!r}) after end of pipeline"
0084:             )
0085: 
0086:         return PipelineNode(name=name_tok.value, statements=stmts, line=name_tok.line)
0087: 
0088:     def parse_statement(self):
0089:         tok = self.peek()
0090:         if tok.type == "LOAD":
0091:             self.advance()
0092:             fname = self.expect("STRING").value.strip('"')
0093:             self.expect("SEMI")
0094:             return LoadStmt(fname, tok.line)
0095: 
0096:         if tok.type == "CLEAN":
0097:             self.advance()
0098:             strategy = self.expect("IDENT").value
0099:             self.expect("SEMI")
0100:             return CleanStmt(strategy, tok.line)
0101: 
0102:         if tok.type == "SELECT":
0103:             self.advance()
0104:             cols = [self.expect("IDENT").value]
0105:             while self.peek().type == "COMMA":
0106:                 self.advance()
0107:                 cols.append(self.expect("IDENT").value)
0108:             self.expect("SEMI")
0109:             return SelectStmt(cols, tok.line)
0110: 
0111:         if tok.type == "DROP":
0112:             self.advance()
0113:             cols = [self.expect("IDENT").value]
0114:             while self.peek().type == "COMMA":
0115:                 self.advance()
0116:                 cols.append(self.expect("IDENT").value)
0117:             self.expect("SEMI")
0118:             return DropStmt(cols, tok.line)
0119: 
0120:         if tok.type == "SPLIT":
0121:             self.advance()
0122:             # 'train' and 'test' are common split names; accept the TRAIN
0123:             # keyword here as a contextual identifier as well as IDENT.
0124:             name_tok = self.peek()
0125:             if name_tok.type in ("IDENT", "TRAIN"):
0126:                 name = self.advance().value
0127:             else:
0128:                 raise SyntaxError_(
0129:                     f"Syntax Error at line {name_tok.line}: expected split "
0130:                     f"name, found {name_tok.type} ({name_tok.value!r})"
0131:                 )
0132:             self.expect("EQUALS")
0133:             pct_tok = self.expect("PERCENT")
0134:             pct = float(pct_tok.value.rstrip("%"))
0135:             self.expect("SEMI")
0136:             return SplitStmt(name, pct, tok.line)
0137: 
0138:         if tok.type == "TRAIN":
0139:             self.advance()
0140:             model = self.expect("IDENT").value
0141:             self.expect("EQUALS")
0142:             algo = self.expect("IDENT").value
0143:             self.expect("SEMI")
0144:             return TrainStmt(model, algo, tok.line)
0145: 
0146:         if tok.type == "EVALUATE":
0147:             self.advance()
0148:             model = self.expect("IDENT").value
0149:             self.expect("SEMI")
0150:             return EvaluateStmt(model, tok.line)
0151: 
0152:         raise SyntaxError_(
0153:             f"Syntax Error at line {tok.line}: unexpected token {tok.type} ({tok.value!r})"
0154:         )

FILE src/ds_pipeline_dsl/semantic.py
0001: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ast_nodes import (
0002:     PipelineNode, LoadStmt, CleanStmt, SelectStmt, DropStmt,
0003:     SplitStmt, TrainStmt, EvaluateStmt
0004: )
0005: 
0006: class SemanticError(Exception):
0007:     pass
0008: 
0009: class SymbolTable:
0010:     """Tracks pipeline state so dependency-based semantic rules can be
0011:     verified, e.g. 'train' cannot appear before 'split' has produced
0012:     training data."""
0013:     def __init__(self):
0014:         self.data_loaded = False
0015:         self.data_cleaned = False
0016:         self.splits: dict = {}
0017:         self.models: dict = {}
0018:         self.load_line = None
0019:         self.split_lines: dict = {}
0020:         self.model_lines: dict = {}
0021:         self.first_train = None
0022:         self.selected_columns = None
0023:         self.dropped_columns: dict = {}
0024: 
0025: def analyze(pipeline: PipelineNode) -> SymbolTable:
0026:     table = SymbolTable()
0027:     for stmt in pipeline.statements:
0028:         if isinstance(stmt, LoadStmt):
0029:             if table.data_loaded:
0030:                 raise SemanticError(
0031:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'load' used more than once; "
0032:                     f"a dataset was already loaded at line {table.load_line}."
0033:                 )
0034:             if stmt.filename.strip() == "":
0035:                 raise SemanticError(
0036:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'load' requires a non-empty filename."
0037:                 )
0038:             table.data_loaded = True
0039:             table.load_line = stmt.line
0040: 
0041:         elif isinstance(stmt, CleanStmt):
0042:             if not table.data_loaded:
0043:                 raise SemanticError(
0044:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'clean' used before "
0045:                     f"any 'load' statement. No dataset is available."
0046:                 )
0047:             if table.first_train:
0048:                 m, t = table.first_train
0049:                 raise SemanticError(
0050:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'clean' used after model '{m}' "
0051:                     f"was trained at line {t}; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'."
0052:                 )
0053:             table.data_cleaned = True
0054: 
0055:         elif isinstance(stmt, SelectStmt):
0056:             if not table.data_loaded:
0057:                 raise SemanticError(
0058:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'select' used before "
0059:                     f"any 'load' statement."
0060:                 )
0061:             if table.first_train:
0062:                 m, t = table.first_train
0063:                 raise SemanticError(
0064:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'select' used after model '{m}' "
0065:                     f"was trained at line {t}; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'."
0066:                 )
0067:             seen_cols = set()
0068:             for col in stmt.columns:
0069:                 if col in seen_cols:
0070:                     raise SemanticError(
0071:                         f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: column '{col}' appears more than once in 'select'."
0072:                     )
0073:                 seen_cols.add(col)
0074: 
0075:             for col in stmt.columns:
0076:                 if col in table.dropped_columns:
0077:                     m = table.dropped_columns[col]
0078:                     raise SemanticError(
0079:                         f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: column '{col}' was dropped at line {m} and cannot be selected."
0080:                     )
0081: 
0082:             table.selected_columns = list(stmt.columns)
0083: 
0084:         elif isinstance(stmt, DropStmt):
0085:             if not table.data_loaded:
0086:                 raise SemanticError(
0087:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'drop' used before "
0088:                     f"any 'load' statement."
0089:                 )
0090:             if table.first_train:
0091:                 m, t = table.first_train
0092:                 raise SemanticError(
0093:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'drop' used after model '{m}' "
0094:                     f"was trained at line {t}; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'."
0095:                 )
0096:             seen_cols = set()
0097:             for col in stmt.columns:
0098:                 if col in seen_cols:
0099:                     raise SemanticError(
0100:                         f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: column '{col}' appears more than once in 'drop'."
0101:                     )
0102:                 seen_cols.add(col)
0103: 
0104:             for col in stmt.columns:
0105:                 if col in table.dropped_columns:
0106:                     m = table.dropped_columns[col]
0107:                     raise SemanticError(
0108:                         f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: column '{col}' was already dropped at line {m}."
0109:                     )
0110: 
0111:             if table.selected_columns is not None:
0112:                 current_selected_set = set(table.selected_columns)
0113:                 for col in stmt.columns:
0114:                     if col not in current_selected_set:
0115:                         raise SemanticError(
0116:                             f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: cannot drop column '{col}': it is not in the current selection."
0117:                         )
0118:                 drop_set = set(stmt.columns)
0119:                 if len(current_selected_set - drop_set) == 0:
0120:                     raise SemanticError(
0121:                         f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'drop' would remove every selected column."
0122:                     )
0123: 
0124:             for col in stmt.columns:
0125:                 table.dropped_columns[col] = stmt.line
0126: 
0127:             if table.selected_columns is not None:
0128:                 drop_set = set(stmt.columns)
0129:                 table.selected_columns = [col for col in table.selected_columns if col not in drop_set]
0130: 
0131:         elif isinstance(stmt, SplitStmt):
0132:             if not table.data_loaded:
0133:                 raise SemanticError(
0134:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: 'split' used before "
0135:                     f"any 'load' statement."
0136:                 )
0137:             if not (0 < stmt.percent < 100):
0138:                 raise SemanticError(
0139:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: split percentage must be strictly between 0 and 100."
0140:                 )
0141:             if stmt.name in table.splits:
0142:                 m = table.split_lines[stmt.name]
0143:                 raise SemanticError(
0144:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: split '{stmt.name}' is already defined at line {m}."
0145:                 )
0146:             total = sum(table.splits.values()) + stmt.percent
0147:             if total > 100 + 1e-9:
0148:                 raise SemanticError(
0149:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: cumulative split total would be {total:g}%, which exceeds 100%."
0150:                 )
0151:             table.splits[stmt.name] = stmt.percent
0152:             table.split_lines[stmt.name] = stmt.line
0153: 
0154:         elif isinstance(stmt, TrainStmt):
0155:             if "train" not in table.splits:
0156:                 raise SemanticError(
0157:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: training data has not "
0158:                     f"been defined. A 'split train = <percent>%;' statement must "
0159:                     f"appear before 'train {stmt.model_name} = {stmt.algorithm};'."
0160:                 )
0161:             if stmt.model_name in table.models:
0162:                 m = table.model_lines[stmt.model_name]
0163:                 raise SemanticError(
0164:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: model '{stmt.model_name}' is already defined at line {m}."
0165:                 )
0166:             if table.first_train is None:
0167:                 table.first_train = (stmt.model_name, stmt.line)
0168:             table.models[stmt.model_name] = stmt.algorithm
0169:             table.model_lines[stmt.model_name] = stmt.line
0170: 
0171:         elif isinstance(stmt, EvaluateStmt):
0172:             if stmt.model_name not in table.models:
0173:                 raise SemanticError(
0174:                     f"Semantic Error at line {stmt.line}: model '{stmt.model_name}' "
0175:                     f"is evaluated before it has been trained."
0176:                 )
0177:     return table

FILE tests/helpers.py
0001: from typing import List, Tuple, Any, Optional
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze
0005: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import lower_to_ir, IRInstruction
0006: 
0007: def compile_to_ir(source: str) -> List[IRInstruction]:
0008:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0009:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0010:     table = analyze(ast)
0011:     return lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0012: 
0013: def _snapshot_state(cleaning_history: List[str],
0014:                     selected_columns: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]],
0015:                     dropped_columns: set,
0016:                     splits: dict) -> Tuple[Tuple[str, ...], Optional[Tuple[str, ...]], frozenset, Tuple[Tuple[str, float], ...]]:
0017:     return (
0018:         tuple(cleaning_history),
0019:         selected_columns,
0020:         frozenset(dropped_columns),
0021:         tuple(sorted(splits.items()))
0022:     )
0023: 
0024: def evaluation_records(ir: List[IRInstruction]) -> List[Tuple[Any, Any, Any, Any]]:
0025:     records = []
0026:     cleaning_history: List[str] = []
0027:     selected_columns: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]] = None
0028:     dropped_columns: set = set()
0029:     splits: dict = {}
0030:     trained_models: dict = {}
0031:     
0032:     last_op = None
0033:     last_clean_strategy = None
0034: 
0035:     for instr in ir:
0036:         op = instr.op
0037:         args = instr.args if instr.args is not None else {}
0038: 
0039:         if op == "OP_CLEAN":
0040:             strat = args.get("strategy")
0041:             # Appends UNLESS immediately preceding interpreted instruction was OP_CLEAN with same strategy
0042:             if not (last_op == "OP_CLEAN" and last_clean_strategy == strat):
0043:                 if strat is not None:
0044:                     cleaning_history.append(strat)
0045:             last_clean_strategy = strat
0046: 
0047:         elif op == "OP_SELECT":
0048:             cols = args.get("columns", [])
0049:             selected_columns = tuple(cols)
0050: 
0051:         elif op == "OP_DROP":
0052:             cols = args.get("columns", [])
0053:             dropped_columns.update(cols)
0054:             if selected_columns is not None:
0055:                 drop_set = set(cols)
0056:                 selected_columns = tuple(c for c in selected_columns if c not in drop_set)
0057: 
0058:         elif op == "OP_SPLIT":
0059:             name = args.get("name")
0060:             pct = args.get("percent")
0061:             if name is not None:
0062:                 splits[name] = pct
0063: 
0064:         elif op == "OP_TRAIN":
0065:             mname = args.get("model_name")
0066:             algo = args.get("algorithm")
0067:             train_st = _snapshot_state(cleaning_history, selected_columns, dropped_columns, splits)
0068:             if mname is not None:
0069:                 trained_models[mname] = (algo, train_st)
0070: 
0071:         elif op == "OP_EVAL":
0072:             mname = args.get("model_name")
0073:             eval_st = _snapshot_state(cleaning_history, selected_columns, dropped_columns, splits)
0074:             if mname in trained_models:
0075:                 algo, train_st = trained_models[mname]
0076:             else:
0077:                 algo, train_st = None, None
0078:             records.append((mname, algo, train_st, eval_st))
0079: 
0080:         last_op = op
0081: 
0082:     return records
0083: 
0084: def assert_order_preserving(unopt: List[IRInstruction], opt: List[IRInstruction]) -> None:
0085:     u_idx = 0
0086:     u_len = len(unopt)
0087:     
0088:     for opt_idx, opt_instr in enumerate(opt):
0089:         found = False
0090:         while u_idx < u_len:
0091:             curr_u = unopt[u_idx]
0092:             
0093:             # Check 1: exact instruction match (same op and args)
0094:             if opt_instr.op == curr_u.op and opt_instr.args == curr_u.args:
0095:                 u_idx += 1
0096:                 found = True
0097:                 break
0098:                 
0099:             # Check 2: merged OP_DROP
0100:             if opt_instr.op == "OP_DROP" and curr_u.op == "OP_DROP":
0101:                 # Check run of contiguous OP_DROP starting at u_idx
0102:                 run_cols = []
0103:                 run_seen = set()
0104:                 run_k = 0
0105:                 while (u_idx + run_k) < u_len and unopt[u_idx + run_k].op == "OP_DROP":
0106:                     for c in unopt[u_idx + run_k].args.get("columns", []):
0107:                         if c not in run_seen:
0108:                             run_seen.add(c)
0109:                             run_cols.append(c)
0110:                     run_k += 1
0111:                     # If columns match opt_instr.args["columns"], we matched the run!
0112:                     if opt_instr.args.get("columns") == run_cols:
0113:                         u_idx += run_k
0114:                         found = True
0115:                         break
0116:                 if found:
0117:                     break
0118: 
0119:             u_idx += 1
0120: 
0121:         assert found, (
0122:             f"Instruction at opt[{opt_idx}] ({opt_instr}) does not map to a strictly increasing "
0123:             f"position in unopt after index {u_idx}"
0124:         )

0027:     counts = bench.expected_counts(n)
0028:     source = bench.build_program(n)
0029: 
0030:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0031:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0032:     table = analyze(ast)
0033:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0034:     notes = []
0035:     opt = optimize(ir, notes)
0036:     trace = execute(opt)
0037: 
0038:     # 1. Statement counts
0039:     assert len(ast.statements) == counts["statements"]
0040:     assert len(ir) == counts["statements"]
0041: 
0042:     # 2. Optimized IR length == 6 + 2*U
0043:     exp_opt = 6 + 2 * counts["U"]
0044:     assert len(opt) == exp_opt
0045:     assert len(opt) < len(ir)  # strictly shorter
0046: 
0047:     # 3. Evaluated model count == U
0048:     eval_lines = [l for l in trace if l.startswith("Evaluated model")]
0049:     assert len(eval_lines) == counts["U"]

0052: @pytest.mark.parametrize("n", [10, 50, 200])
0053: def test_structural_validity_from_ast_independent_of_analyze(n):
0054:     source = bench.build_program(n)
0055:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0056:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0057: 
0058:     # Rule 1: Exactly one LoadStmt
0059:     load_stmts = [s for s in ast.statements if isinstance(s, LoadStmt)]
0060:     assert len(load_stmts) == 1
0061: 
0062:     # Find the index of the first TrainStmt
0063:     first_train_idx = next(
0064:         (idx for idx, s in enumerate(ast.statements) if isinstance(s, TrainStmt)),
0065:         None
0066:     )
0067:     assert first_train_idx is not None
0068: 
0069:     # Rule 2: No CleanStmt, SelectStmt, or DropStmt after the first TrainStmt
0070:     for s in ast.statements[first_train_idx:]:
0071:         assert not isinstance(s, (CleanStmt, SelectStmt, DropStmt)), (
0072:             f"Found forbidden data preparation statement {type(s)} after first train at statement {s}"
0073:         )
0074: 
0075:     # Rule 3: All DropStmt precede the first SelectStmt
0076:     first_select_idx = next(
0077:         (idx for idx, s in enumerate(ast.statements) if isinstance(s, SelectStmt)),
0078:         None
0079:     )
0080:     assert first_select_idx is not None
0081:     for idx, s in enumerate(ast.statements):
0082:         if isinstance(s, DropStmt):
0083:             assert idx < first_select_idx, "DropStmt found at or after first SelectStmt"
0084: 
0085:     # Rule 4: Model names are unique
0086:     train_stmts = [s for s in ast.statements if isinstance(s, TrainStmt)]
0087:     model_names = [s.model_name for s in train_stmts]
0088:     assert len(model_names) == len(set(model_names)), "Duplicate model name detected"
0089: 
0090:     # Rule 5: Split names unique and percent total <= 100
0091:     split_stmts = [s for s in ast.statements if isinstance(s, SplitStmt)]
0092:     split_names = [s.name for s in split_stmts]
0093:     assert len(split_names) == len(set(split_names)), "Duplicate split name detected"
0094:     total_percent = sum(s.percent for s in split_stmts)
0095:     assert total_percent <= 100.0 + 1e-9, f"Total split percent {total_percent} exceeds 100"
0096: 
0097:     # Rule 6: Dropped names disjoint from selected names
0098:     dropped_cols = set()
0099:     for s in ast.statements:
0100:         if isinstance(s, DropStmt):
0101:             dropped_cols.update(s.columns)
0102: 
0103:     selected_cols = set()
0104:     for s in ast.statements:
0105:         if isinstance(s, SelectStmt):
0106:             selected_cols.update(s.columns)
0107: 
0108:     assert dropped_cols.isdisjoint(selected_cols), (
0109:         f"Overlap between dropped and selected columns: {dropped_cols & selected_cols}"
0110:     )
0111: 
0112:     # Rule 7: Every EvaluateStmt model has an earlier TrainStmt
0113:     trained_so_far = set()
0114:     for s in ast.statements:
0115:         if isinstance(s, TrainStmt):
0116:             trained_so_far.add(s.model_name)
0117:         elif isinstance(s, EvaluateStmt):
0118:             assert s.model_name in trained_so_far, (
0119:                 f"Model '{s.model_name}' evaluated before it was trained"
0120:             )
0121:

0065:     assert trace[-1] == "Evaluated model 'm' (simulated accuracy trace)"

0067: def test_boundary_no_trailing_newline():

0071:     table = analyze(ast)

0077:     cols = [f"c_{i}" for i in range(200)]

0084:     cols = [f"c_{i}" for i in range(200)]

0090: def test_boundary_identifier_5000_characters():
0091:     long_name = "model_" + "a" * 5000
0092:     source = f'pipeline p {{ load "d.csv"; split train = 80%; train {long_name} = dt; evaluate {long_name}; }}'
0093:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0094:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0095:     table = analyze(ast)
0096:     assert long_name in table.models
0097:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)

0099:     assert f"Trained model '{long_name}' using dt" in trace
0100:     assert f"Evaluated model '{long_name}' (simulated accuracy trace)" in trace
0101: 
0102: def test_boundary_1000_statements_program():
0103:     clean_stmts = "\n    ".join("clean strat;" for _ in range(996))
0104:     source = f'''pipeline p {{
0105:     load "d.csv";
0106:     {clean_stmts}
0107:     split train = 80%;
0108:     train m = dt;
0109:     evaluate m;
0110: }}'''
0111:     ir = compile_to_ir(source)
0112:     assert len(ir) == 1000
0113:     opt = optimize(ir)
0114:     # The 996 duplicate clean statements will collapse into 1 clean statement!
0115:     # Result: load, 1 clean, split, train, eval = 5 instructions
0116:     assert len(opt) == 5
0117:     trace = execute(opt)
0118:     assert len(trace) == 5
0119: 
0120: def test_boundary_pipeline_with_only_load():
0121:     source = 'pipeline p { load "dataset.csv"; }'
0122:     ir = compile_to_ir(source)
0123:     opt = optimize(ir)
0124:     assert len(opt) == 1
0125:     assert opt[0].op == "OP_LOAD"
0126:     trace = execute(opt)
0127:     assert trace == ["Loaded dataset.csv"]
0128: 
0129: def test_boundary_pipeline_with_no_evaluate_preserves_trains():
0130:     source = 'pipeline p { load "d.csv"; split train = 80%; train m = dt; }'
0131:     ir = compile_to_ir(source)
0132:     opt = optimize(ir)
0133:     train_inst = [i for i in opt if i.op == "OP_TRAIN"]
0134:     assert len(train_inst) == 1
0135:     assert train_inst[0].args["model_name"] == "m"
0136:     trace = execute(opt)
0137:     assert "Trained model 'm' using dt" in trace
0138:     assert not any("Evaluated model" in t for t in trace)

0025: EXPECTED = {name: outcome for name, outcome, _ in EXAMPLES_TABLE}
0026: EXAMPLES = sorted((ROOT / "examples").glob("*.pipe"))
0027: ERROR_TYPES = {LexicalError: "Lexical", SyntaxError_: "Syntax", SemanticError: "Semantic"}
0028: ERROR_SOURCES = (
0029:     ("Lexical", 'pipeline p {\n @\n}'),
0030:     ("Syntax", 'pipeline p {\n load "d.csv"\n}'),
0031:     ("Semantic", 'pipeline p {\n clean missing;\n}'),

0035: @pytest.mark.parametrize("path", EXAMPLES, ids=lambda p: p.name)
0036: def test_runner_matches_direct_compiler_for_every_example(path):
0037:     source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
0038:     result = runner.compile_source(source)
0039:     direct = {}
0040:     try:
0041:         with redirect_stdout(StringIO()):
0042:             direct["tokens"] = tokenize(source)
0043:             direct["ast"] = Parser(direct["tokens"]).parse_pipeline()
0044:             direct["symbol_table"] = analyze(direct["ast"])
0045:             direct["ir"] = lower_to_ir(direct["ast"], direct["symbol_table"])
0046:             direct["notes"] = []
0047:             direct["optimized_ir"] = optimize(direct["ir"], direct["notes"])
0048:             direct["trace"] = execute(direct["optimized_ir"])
0049:     except tuple(ERROR_TYPES) as exc:
0050:         assert EXPECTED[path.name] == ERROR_TYPES[type(exc)].lower()
0051:         assert result.error.kind == ERROR_TYPES[type(exc)]
0052:         assert result.error.message == str(exc)
0053:         assert result.error.stage == result.failed_stage
0054:         if result.error.line is not None:
0055:             assert 1 <= result.error.line <= len(source.splitlines()) + 1
0056:         failed = next(i for i, stage in enumerate(result.stages) if stage.status == "failed")
0057:         assert all(s.status == "ok" for s in result.stages[:failed])
0058:         assert all(s.status == "skipped" for s in result.stages[failed + 1:])
0059:         if result.error.kind == "Syntax":
0060:             assert result.tokens
0061:         if result.error.kind == "Semantic":
0062:             assert result.ast is not None
0063:     else:
0064:         assert EXPECTED[path.name] == "ok"
0065:         assert result.error is None
0066:         assert all(s.status == "ok" for s in result.stages)
0067:         assert result.ir == direct["ir"]
0068:         assert result.optimized_ir == direct["optimized_ir"]
0069:         assert result.notes == direct["notes"]
0070:         assert result.trace == direct["trace"]
0071:         assert result.unoptimized_trace == execute(direct["ir"])
0072:         assert vars(result.symbol_table) == vars(direct["symbol_table"])
0073:         assert result.ir_before == len(direct["ir"])
0074:         assert result.ir_after == len(direct["optimized_ir"])
0075:         assert result.removed == result.ir_before - result.ir_after
0076:         assert result.reduction_pct == pytest.approx(100 * result.removed / result.ir_before)
0077:     if "tokens" in direct:
0078:         assert [asdict(t) for t in result.tokens] == [asdict(t) for t in direct["tokens"]]
0079:         assert result.tokens[-1].type == "EOF"
0080:     if "ast" in direct:
0081:         assert repr(result.ast) == repr(direct["ast"])

0141:     real_tokenize = runner.tokenize
0142:     def noisy_tokenize(source):
0143:         print("stray lexer output")
0144:         return real_tokenize(source)
0145:     monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "tokenize", noisy_tokenize)
0146:     assert runner.compile_source("pipeline p {}").error is None
0147:     def broken(*args):
0148:         print("stray semantic output")
0149:         raise RuntimeError("unexpected internal failure")

0159: def test_unoptimized_comparison_cannot_break_result(monkeypatch, capsys):
0160:     real_execute = runner.execute
0161:     calls = []
0162:     def execute_once(ir):
0163:         calls.append(ir)
0164:         print("stray executor output")
0165:         if len(calls) == 2:
0166:             raise RuntimeError("comparison unavailable")
0167:         return real_execute(ir)
0168:     monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "execute", execute_once)
0169:     result = runner.compile_source("pipeline p {}")
0170:     assert result.error is None
0171:     assert result.stages[-1].status == "ok"
0172:     assert result.unoptimized_trace is None
0173:     assert "comparison unavailable" in result.comparison_error
0174:     assert capsys.readouterr().out == ""

0193: def test_performance_loader_header_driven_and_verbatim(tmp_path):

0197:               "| Status | IR after | Statements | us/stmt | Lexer ms | Reduction % |\n"
0198:               "|:---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n"

0201:               "| reduced repetitions | 70 | 1000 | 2.4 | .2 | 30% |\n\n"
0202:               "## Observations\n1. Exact *text*.\n\n## Next\nexcluded\n")

0212:     assert data.observations == "1. Exact *text*.\n\n"
0213:     assert data.marked_rows == [1, 2]
0214:     # Preserve CRLF section text too.
0215:     with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
0216:         handle.write(report.replace("\n", "\r\n"))
0217:     assert load_performance(path).environment == "- Python: test\r\n\r\n"

0318:     monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "read_example", unreadable)
0319:     app.selectbox(key="example_choice").select("optimizer_demo.pipe").run()
0320:     assert not app.exception
0321:     assert any("Could not read" in w.value for w in app.warning)
0322:     import performance_loader
0323:     monkeypatch.setattr(performance_loader, "load_performance", lambda p: performance_loader.PerformanceData(False, "missing report"))
0324:     app.sidebar.radio(key="page").set_value("Performance").run()
0325:     assert not app.exception
0326:     assert any("python benchmarks/benchmark.py" in w.value for w in app.warning)
0327: 
0328: 
0329: def test_ui_empty_oversized_input_and_large_token_stream(app):
0330:     playground(app)
0331:     for source, kind in (("", "Syntax"), (" " * (runner.MAX_SOURCE_CHARS + 1), "Input")):
0332:         app.text_area(key="editor_text").set_value(source).run()
0333:         app.button(key="compile_btn").click().run()
0334:         assert not app.exception
0335:         assert any(kind + " Error" in e.value for e in app.error)
0336:     source = 'pipeline p { load "d.csv";\n' + 'clean x;\n' * 350 + '}'
0337:     app.text_area(key="editor_text").set_value(source).run()
0338:     app.button(key="compile_btn").click().run()
0339:     assert not app.exception
0340:     assert any("showing first 1000 of" in c.value for c in app.caption)
0341:     assert any("Final token" in c.value and "EOF" in c.value for c in app.caption)

FILE tests/test_examples.py
0001: import pytest
0002: import os
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize, LexicalError
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser, SyntaxError_
0005: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze, SemanticError
0006: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import lower_to_ir
0007: from ds_pipeline_dsl.optimizer import optimize
0008: from ds_pipeline_dsl.executor import execute
0009: 
0010: def get_example_path(filename):
0011:     return os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', 'examples', filename)
0012: 
0013: def run_pipeline(filepath):
0014:     with open(filepath, 'r') as f:
0015:         code = f.read()
0016:     tokens = tokenize(code)
0017:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0018:     table = analyze(ast)
0019:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0020:     opt_ir = optimize(ir)
0021:     return execute(opt_ir)
0022:

0024:     trace = run_pipeline(get_example_path('valid_basic.pipe'))
0025:     assert len(trace) == 6
0026:     assert trace[0] == "Loaded students.csv"
0027:     assert trace[1] == "Cleaned using missing_values"
0028:     assert trace[2] == "Selected columns: cgpa, attendance, study_hours"
0029:     assert trace[3] == "Defined split 'train' = 80%"
0030:     assert trace[4] == "Trained model 'model' using decision_tree"
0031:     assert trace[5] == "Evaluated model 'model' (simulated accuracy trace)"

0121:     ("valid_drop.pipe", "ok", "Dropped columns: student_id, name"),
0122:     ("valid_select_then_drop.pipe", "ok", "Dropped columns: extra_col"),
0123:     ("optimizer_demo.pipe", "ok", "Evaluated model 'model1'"),

0127:     ("invalid_clean_after_train.pipe", "semantic", "'clean' used after model 'model' was trained"),
0128:     ("invalid_drop_after_train.pipe", "semantic", "'drop' used after model 'model' was trained"),
0129:     ("invalid_drop_all_selected.pipe", "semantic", "'drop' would remove every selected column"),
0130:     ("invalid_drop_before_load.pipe", "semantic", "'drop' used before any 'load' statement"),
0131:     ("invalid_drop_twice.pipe", "semantic", "was already dropped at line"),
0132:     ("invalid_duplicate_load.pipe", "semantic", "'load' used more than once"),
0133:     ("invalid_duplicate_model.pipe", "semantic", "is already defined at line"),
0134:     ("invalid_duplicate_split.pipe", "semantic", "is already defined at line"),
0135:     ("invalid_lexical_error.pipe", "lexical", "unexpected character"),
0136:     ("invalid_select_dropped_column.pipe", "semantic", "was dropped at line 4 and cannot be selected"),
0137:     ("invalid_split_total_over_100.pipe", "semantic", "cumulative split total would be 110%, which exceeds 100%"),
0138:     ("invalid_syntax_error.pipe", "syntax", "Syntax Error"),
0139:     ("invalid_train_before_split.pipe", "semantic", "training data has not been defined"),
0140: ]
0141: 
0142: def test_all_examples_covered_by_table():
0143:     examples_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', 'examples')
0144:     all_pipe_files = {f for f in os.listdir(examples_dir) if f.endswith('.pipe')}
0145:     table_files = {entry[0] for entry in EXAMPLES_TABLE}
0146:     missing = all_pipe_files - table_files
0147:     assert not missing, f"The following .pipe files in examples/ are missing from EXAMPLES_TABLE: {missing}"
0148: 
0149: @pytest.mark.parametrize("filename,outcome,substring", EXAMPLES_TABLE)
0150: def test_example_from_table(filename, outcome, substring):
0151:     filepath = get_example_path(filename)
0152:     if outcome == "ok":
0153:         trace = run_pipeline(filepath)
0154:         assert any(substring in line for line in trace), f"Expected substring '{substring}' in trace: {trace}"
0155:     elif outcome == "lexical":
0156:         with pytest.raises(LexicalError) as exc_info:
0157:             run_pipeline(filepath)
0158:         assert substring in str(exc_info.value)
0159:     elif outcome == "syntax":
0160:         with pytest.raises(SyntaxError_) as exc_info:
0161:             run_pipeline(filepath)
0162:         assert substring in str(exc_info.value)
0163:     elif outcome == "semantic":
0164:         with pytest.raises(SemanticError) as exc_info:
0165:             run_pipeline(filepath)
0166:         assert substring in str(exc_info.value)
0167:     else:
0168:         pytest.fail(f"Unknown expected outcome: {outcome}")
0169: 

FILE tests/test_executor.py
0001: import pytest
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze
0005: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import lower_to_ir
0006: from ds_pipeline_dsl.optimizer import optimize
0007: from ds_pipeline_dsl.executor import execute
0008: 
0009: VALID_PROGRAM = '''
0010: pipeline student_prediction {
0011:     load "students.csv";
0012:     clean missing_values;
0013:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0014:     split train = 80%;
0015:     train model = decision_tree;
0016:     evaluate model;
0017: }
0018: '''
0019: 
0020: def test_executor():
0021:     tokens = tokenize(VALID_PROGRAM)
0022:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0023:     table = analyze(ast)
0024:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0025:     opt_ir = optimize(ir)
0026:     
0027:     trace = execute(opt_ir)
0028:     
0029:     assert len(trace) == 6
0030:     assert trace[0] == "Loaded students.csv"
0031:     assert trace[1] == "Cleaned using missing_values"
0032:     assert trace[2] == "Selected columns: cgpa, attendance, study_hours"
0033:     assert trace[3] == "Defined split 'train' = 80%"
0034:     assert trace[4] == "Trained model 'model' using decision_tree"
0035:     assert trace[5] == "Evaluated model 'model' (simulated accuracy trace)"
0036: 
0037: def test_executor_with_drop():
0038:     program = '''
0039: pipeline p {
0040:     load "students.csv";
0041:     drop a, b;
0042:     select c;
0043: }
0044: '''
0045:     tokens = tokenize(program)
0046:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0047:     table = analyze(ast)
0048:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0049:     trace = execute(ir)
0050:     assert "Dropped columns: a, b" in trace
0051:     assert trace[1] == "Dropped columns: a, b"

0017: EXAMPLES_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "examples")

0020:     return os.path.join(EXAMPLES_DIR, filename)
0021: 
0022: def run_cli_args(args: list) -> tuple[int, str]:
0023:     test_args = ["cli.py"] + args
0024:     with patch.object(sys, 'argv', test_args):
0025:         with patch('sys.stdout', new=StringIO()) as fake_out:
0026:             code = 0
0027:             try:
0028:                 main()
0029:             except SystemExit as e:
0030:                 code = e.code
0031:             return code, fake_out.getvalue()

0034: # ==============================================================================
0035: # COMPLEX PIPELINE EXAMPLES & EQUIVALENCE
0036: # ==============================================================================
0037: 
0038: def test_complex_multi_model():
0039:     path = get_example_path("complex_multi_model.pipe")
0040:     with open(path, "r") as f:
0041:         src = f.read()
0042: 
0043:     unopt = compile_to_ir(src)
0044:     notes = []
0045:     opt = optimize(unopt, notes)
0046: 
0047:     # 1. Snapshot / record equivalence
0048:     assert evaluation_records(unopt) == evaluation_records(opt)
0049:     assert_order_preserving(unopt, opt)
0050: 
0051:     # 2. Assert both evaluations survive optimization
0052:     evals = [i for i in opt if i.op == "OP_EVAL"]
0053:     assert len(evals) == 2
0054:     assert evals[0].args["model_name"] == "m1"
0055:     assert evals[1].args["model_name"] == "m2"
0056: 
0057:     # 3. Dead train is removed
0058:     train_models = [i.args["model_name"] for i in opt if i.op == "OP_TRAIN"]
0059:     assert "m_dead" not in train_models
0060:     assert train_models == ["m1", "m2"]
0061:     assert any("dead-train: removed OP_TRAIN 'm_dead'" in n for n in notes)
0062: 
0063:     # 4. Trace has exactly 2 "Evaluated model" lines in order
0064:     trace = execute(opt)
0065:     eval_lines = [l for l in trace if l.startswith("Evaluated model")]
0066:     assert eval_lines == [
0067:         "Evaluated model 'm1' (simulated accuracy trace)",
0068:         "Evaluated model 'm2' (simulated accuracy trace)",
0069:     ]
0070: 
0071:     # 5. CLI runs with exit code 0
0072:     code, output = run_cli_args([path, "--trace"])
0073:     assert code == 0
0074:     assert "Evaluated model 'm1'" in output
0075:     assert "Evaluated model 'm2'" in output
0076:

FILE tests/test_ir.py
0001: import pytest
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze
0005: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import lower_to_ir, IRInstruction
0006: 
0007: VALID_PROGRAM = '''
0008: pipeline student_prediction {
0009:     load "students.csv";
0010:     clean missing_values;
0011:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0012:     split train = 80%;
0013:     train model = decision_tree;
0014:     evaluate model;
0015: }
0016: '''
0017: 
0018: def test_lower_to_ir():
0019:     tokens = tokenize(VALID_PROGRAM)
0020:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0021:     table = analyze(ast)
0022:     
0023:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0024:     
0025:     assert len(ir) == 6
0026:     assert ir[0].op == "OP_LOAD"
0027:     assert ir[0].args["filename"] == "students.csv"
0028:     
0029:     assert ir[1].op == "OP_CLEAN"
0030:     assert ir[1].args["strategy"] == "missing_values"
0031:     
0032:     assert ir[2].op == "OP_SELECT"
0033:     assert ir[2].args["columns"] == ["cgpa", "attendance", "study_hours"]
0034:     
0035:     assert ir[3].op == "OP_SPLIT"
0036:     assert ir[3].args["name"] == "train"
0037:     assert ir[3].args["percent"] == 80.0
0038:     
0039:     assert ir[4].op == "OP_TRAIN"
0040:     assert ir[4].args["model_name"] == "model"
0041:     assert ir[4].args["algorithm"] == "decision_tree"
0042:     
0043:     assert ir[5].op == "OP_EVAL"
0044:     assert ir[5].args["model_name"] == "model"
0045: 
0046: def test_lower_to_ir_with_drop():
0047:     program = '''
0048: pipeline p {
0049:     load "d.csv";
0050:     drop id, name;
0051:     select cgpa;
0052: }
0053: '''
0054:     tokens = tokenize(program)
0055:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0056:     table = analyze(ast)
0057:     ir = lower_to_ir(ast, table)
0058:     assert len(ir) == 3
0059:     assert ir[0].op == "OP_LOAD"
0060:     assert ir[1].op == "OP_DROP"
0061:     assert ir[1].args["columns"] == ["id", "name"]
0062:     assert ir[1].args["line"] == 4
0063:     assert ir[2].op == "OP_SELECT"

FILE tests/test_lexer.py
0001: import pytest
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize, Token, LexicalError
0003: 
0004: def test_tokenize_valid():
0005:     source = 'pipeline my_pipe { load "data.csv"; }'
0006:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0007:     assert len(tokens) == 8
0008:     assert tokens[0].type == "PIPELINE"
0009:     assert tokens[1].type == "IDENT"
0010:     assert tokens[1].value == "my_pipe"
0011: 
0012: def test_tokenize_invalid():
0013:     source = 'pipeline @my_pipe { }'
0014:     with pytest.raises(LexicalError):
0015:         tokenize(source)
0016: 
0017: def test_tokenize_crlf_vs_lf():
0018:     lf_source = 'pipeline my_pipe {\n    load "data.csv";\n    clean missing;\n}\n'
0019:     crlf_source = 'pipeline my_pipe {\r\n    load "data.csv";\r\n    clean missing;\r\n}\r\n'
0020:     tokens_lf = tokenize(lf_source)
0021:     tokens_crlf = tokenize(crlf_source)
0022:     assert len(tokens_lf) == len(tokens_crlf)
0023:     for t_lf, t_crlf in zip(tokens_lf, tokens_crlf):
0024:         assert t_lf.type == t_crlf.type
0025:         assert t_lf.value == t_crlf.value
0026:         assert t_lf.line == t_crlf.line
0027:         assert t_lf.col == t_crlf.col
0028: 
0029: def test_tokenize_unterminated_string():
0030:     source = 'pipeline my_pipe { load "data.csv; }'
0031:     with pytest.raises(LexicalError) as exc_info:
0032:         tokenize(source)
0033:     assert "unterminated string literal" in str(exc_info.value)
0034: 
0035: def test_tokenize_string_with_comment_slashes():
0036:     source = 'load "path//to//data.csv";'
0037:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0038:     str_tokens = [t for t in tokens if t.type == "STRING"]
0039:     assert len(str_tokens) == 1
0040:     assert str_tokens[0].value == '"path//to//data.csv"'
0041: 
0042: def test_tokenize_multiline_string_unterminated():
0043:     source = 'load "line1\nline2";'
0044:     with pytest.raises(LexicalError) as exc_info:
0045:         tokenize(source)
0046:     assert "unterminated string literal" in str(exc_info.value)
0047: 
0048: def test_tokenize_drop_keyword():
0049:     source = 'drop col1, col2;'
0050:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0051:     assert tokens[0].type == "DROP"
0052:     assert tokens[0].value == "drop"
0053:     assert tokens[1].type == "IDENT"
0054:     assert tokens[1].value == "col1"
0055: 
0056: def test_tokenize_droplet_identifier():
0057:     source = 'select droplet;'
0058:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0059:     assert tokens[1].type == "IDENT"
0060:     assert tokens[1].value == "droplet"
0061:

0063: def test_tokenize_illegal_characters(illegal_char):

0065:     with pytest.raises(LexicalError) as exc_info:
0066:         tokenize(source)
0067:     assert f"unexpected character {illegal_char!r}" in str(exc_info.value)

0098:     source = f"select {ident};"

0103: def test_tokenize_line_and_col_numbers_line3_plus():
0104:     source = 'load "d.csv";\n\nclean missing;\n'
0105:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0106:     clean_tok = next(t for t in tokens if t.value == "clean")
0107:     missing_tok = next(t for t in tokens if t.value == "missing")
0108:     assert clean_tok.line == 3
0109:     assert clean_tok.col == 0
0110:     assert missing_tok.line == 3
0111:     assert missing_tok.col == 6

0081: VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE = """pipeline MultiModel {
0082:     load "data.csv";
0083:     clean missing;
0084:     clean outliers;
0085:     clean missing;
0086:     select a, b, c;
0087:     drop c;
0088:     split train = 80%;
0089:     train m1 = dt;
0090:     evaluate m1;
0091:     train m2 = rf;
0092:     evaluate m2;
0093: }
0094: """
0095: 
0096: def test_oracle_sensitivity_delete_live_train():
0097:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0098:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0099:     mutated = [i for i in ir if not (i.op == "OP_TRAIN" and i.args.get("model_name") == "m1")]
0100:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs
0101: 
0102: def test_oracle_sensitivity_delete_split():
0103:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0104:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0105:     mutated = [i for i in ir if i.op != "OP_SPLIT"]
0106:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs
0107: 
0108: def test_oracle_sensitivity_truncate_after_first_eval():
0109:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0110:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0111:     first_eval_idx = next(idx for idx, i in enumerate(ir) if i.op == "OP_EVAL")
0112:     mutated = ir[:first_eval_idx + 1]
0113:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs
0114: 
0115: def test_oracle_sensitivity_remove_non_adjacent_clean():
0116:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0117:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0118:     clean_indices = [idx for idx, i in enumerate(ir) if i.op == "OP_CLEAN"]
0119:     assert len(clean_indices) == 3
0120:     # Remove the second non-adjacent 'clean missing'
0121:     mutated = [i for idx, i in enumerate(ir) if idx != clean_indices[2]]
0122:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs
0123: 
0124: def test_oracle_sensitivity_reorder_cleans():
0125:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0126:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0127:     clean_indices = [idx for idx, i in enumerate(ir) if i.op == "OP_CLEAN"]
0128:     mutated = list(ir)
0129:     mutated[clean_indices[0]], mutated[clean_indices[1]] = mutated[clean_indices[1]], mutated[clean_indices[0]]
0130:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs
0131: 
0132: def test_oracle_sensitivity_delete_drop():
0133:     ir = compile_to_ir(VALID_MULTI_MODEL_SOURCE)
0134:     orig_recs = evaluation_records(ir)
0135:     mutated = [i for i in ir if i.op != "OP_DROP"]
0136:     assert evaluation_records(mutated) != orig_recs

0485: EQUIVALENCE_PIPELINES = {
0486:     "dead_train": """pipeline DeadTrain {
0487:         load "data.csv";
0488:         split train = 80%;
0489:         train m1 = dt;
0490:         train m2 = rf;
0491:         evaluate m2;
0492:     }""",
0493:     "trailing_train_split": """pipeline Trailing {
0494:         load "data.csv";
0495:         split train = 80%;
0496:         train m1 = dt;
0497:         evaluate m1;
0498:         split test = 20%;
0499:         train m2 = rf;
0500:     }""",
0501:     "duplicate_cleans": """pipeline DupCleans {
0502:         load "data.csv";
0503:         clean missing;
0504:         clean missing;
0505:         select a, b;
0506:         split train = 80%;
0507:         train m1 = dt;
0508:         evaluate m1;
0509:     }""",
0510:     "adjacent_drops": """pipeline AdjDrops {
0511:         load "data.csv";
0512:         select a, b, c, d;
0513:         drop a;
0514:         drop b, c;
0515:         split train = 80%;
0516:         train m1 = dt;
0517:         evaluate m1;
0518:     }""",
0519:     "multi_model": """pipeline MultiModel {
0520:         load "data.csv";
0521:         clean missing;
0522:         select a, b;
0523:         split train = 50%;
0524:         train m1 = dt;
0525:         evaluate m1;
0526:         split test = 20%;
0527:         train m2 = rf;
0528:         evaluate m2;
0529:         train m3 = svm;
0530:         evaluate m3;
0531:     }""",
0532:     "key_split_after_train": """pipeline KeySplitAfterTrain {
0533:         load "data.csv";
0534:         split train = 70%;
0535:         train m1 = dt;
0536:         evaluate m1;
0537:         split test = 30%;
0538:         train m2 = rf;
0539:         evaluate m2;
0540:     }""",
0541:     "eval_twice": """pipeline EvalTwice {
0542:         load "data.csv";
0543:         clean missing;
0544:         split train = 80%;
0545:         train m1 = dt;
0546:         evaluate m1;
0547:         evaluate m1;
0548:     }""",
0549:     "select_then_drop": """pipeline SelectThenDrop {
0550:         load "data.csv";
0551:         select a, b, c;
0552:         drop c;
0553:         split train = 80%;
0554:         train m1 = dt;
0555:         evaluate m1;
0556:     }""",
0557:     "no_eval": """pipeline NoEval {
0558:         load "data.csv";
0559:         clean missing;
0560:         select a, b;
0561:         split train = 80%;
0562:         train m1 = dt;
0563:     }""",
0564:     "optimizer_demo": open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "examples", "optimizer_demo.pipe")).read(),
0565: }
0566: 
0567: def _assert_subsequence(sub: list, full: list) -> None:
0568:     it = iter(full)
0569:     for item in sub:
0570:         assert item in it, f"'{item}' not in remainder of full trace"
0571: 
0572: @pytest.mark.parametrize("name,source", list(EQUIVALENCE_PIPELINES.items()))
0573: def test_pipeline_equivalence_full_and_passes(name, source):
0574:     unopt = compile_to_ir(source)
0575:     notes = []
0576:     opt = optimize(unopt, notes)
0577: 
0578:     # 1. Observable evaluation record equality
0579:     unopt_recs = evaluation_records(unopt)
0580:     opt_recs = evaluation_records(opt)
0581:     assert unopt_recs == opt_recs, f"Pipeline {name}: evaluation_records differ"
0582: 
0583:     # 2. Pass-level record equality (each pass alone)
0584:     for pass_fn in [_remove_unreachable, _remove_dead_trains, _merge_adjacent_drops, _collapse_duplicates]:
0585:         pass_opt = pass_fn(unopt)
0586:         assert evaluation_records(pass_opt) == unopt_recs, f"Pipeline {name}, pass {pass_fn.__name__} failed equivalence"
0587: 
0588:     # 3. Order-preserving two-pointer check
0589:     assert_order_preserving(unopt, opt)
0590: 
0591:     # 4. Executor trace checks
0592:     unopt_trace = execute(unopt)
0593:     opt_trace = execute(opt)
0594: 
0595:     eval_unopt = [l for l in unopt_trace if l.startswith("Evaluated model")]
0596:     eval_opt = [l for l in opt_trace if l.startswith("Evaluated model")]
0597:     assert eval_unopt == eval_opt, f"Pipeline {name}: 'Evaluated model' lines differ"
0598: 
0599:     trace_sub = [l for l in opt_trace if not l.startswith("Dropped columns")]
0600:     trace_full = [l for l in unopt_trace if not l.startswith("Dropped columns")]
0601:     _assert_subsequence(trace_sub, trace_full)
0602: 
0603:     drop_cols_unopt = set()
0604:     for l in unopt_trace:
0605:         if l.startswith("Dropped columns: "):
0606:             drop_cols_unopt.update(l.removeprefix("Dropped columns: ").split(", "))
0607: 
0608:     drop_cols_opt = set()
0609:     for l in opt_trace:
0610:         if l.startswith("Dropped columns: "):
0611:             drop_cols_opt.update(l.removeprefix("Dropped columns: ").split(", "))
0612: 
0613:     assert drop_cols_unopt == drop_cols_opt, f"Pipeline {name}: union of dropped columns differ"
0614: 
0615: 
0616: # ==============================================================================
0617: # CATEGORY E: Idempotence Tests
0618: # ==============================================================================
0619: 
0620: @pytest.mark.parametrize("name,source", list(EQUIVALENCE_PIPELINES.items()))
0621: def test_pipeline_idempotence(name, source):
0622:     unopt = compile_to_ir(source)
0623:     opt1 = optimize(unopt)
0624:     opt2 = optimize(opt1)
0625:     assert len(opt1) == len(opt2), f"Pipeline {name}: lengths differ after second optimize"
0626:     for i1, i2 in zip(opt1, opt2):
0627:         assert i1.op == i2.op, f"Pipeline {name}: opcode differ: {i1.op} vs {i2.op}"
0628:         assert i1.args == i2.args, f"Pipeline {name}: args differ: {i1.args} vs {i2.args}"
0629:

0640:             main()
0641:             output = fake_out.getvalue()

0646:     assert "drop-merge:" in output
0647:     assert "duplicate:" in output
0648:     assert "IR instructions: 11 -> 7" in output

0001: import pytest
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser, SyntaxError_

0006: def test_parser_valid():
0007:     source = 'pipeline my_pipe { load "data.csv"; }'
0008:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0009:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0010:     assert isinstance(ast, PipelineNode)
0011:     assert ast.name == "my_pipe"
0012:     assert len(ast.statements) == 1
0013:     assert isinstance(ast.statements[0], LoadStmt)
0014:     assert ast.statements[0].filename == "data.csv"
0015:

0017:     source = 'pipeline my_pipe { load ; }'

0019:     with pytest.raises(SyntaxError_):
0020:         Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0021: 
0022: def test_parser_trailing_tokens():
0023:     source = 'pipeline p { load "data.csv"; } extra;'
0024:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0025:     with pytest.raises(SyntaxError_) as exc_info:
0026:         Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0027:     assert "unexpected token" in str(exc_info.value)
0028:     assert "after end of pipeline" in str(exc_info.value)
0029:

0167:     assert ast.statements[2].percent == 20.0

FILE tests/test_semantic.py
0001: import pytest
0002: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0003: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser
0004: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze, SemanticError
0005: 
0006: VALID_PROGRAM = '''
0007: pipeline student_prediction {
0008:     load "students.csv";
0009:     clean missing_values;
0010:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0011:     split train = 80%;
0012:     train model = decision_tree;
0013:     evaluate model;
0014: }
0015: '''
0016: 
0017: INVALID_PROGRAM = '''
0018: pipeline student_prediction {
0019:     load "students.csv";
0020:     clean missing_values;
0021:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0022:     train model = decision_tree;
0023:     evaluate model;
0024: }
0025: '''
0026: 
0027: def test_semantic_valid():
0028:     tokens = tokenize(VALID_PROGRAM)
0029:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0030:     table = analyze(ast)
0031:     assert table.data_loaded is True
0032:     assert table.data_cleaned is True
0033:     assert "train" in table.splits
0034:     assert table.splits["train"] == 80.0
0035:     assert "model" in table.models

0041:         analyze(ast)

FILE examples/complex_multi_model.pipe
0001: pipeline complex_multi_model {
0002:     load "students.csv";
0003:     drop id, notes;
0004:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours, exam_score;
0005:     split train = 70%;
0006:     split test = 30%;
0007:     train m_dead = logistic_regression;
0008:     train m1 = decision_tree;
0009:     evaluate m1;
0010:     train m2 = random_forest;
0011:     evaluate m2;
0012: }

FILE examples/complex_redundant.pipe
0001: pipeline complex_redundant {
0002:     load "students.csv";
0003:     clean missing_values;
0004:     clean missing_values;
0005:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours, col_a, col_b;
0006:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours, col_a, col_b;
0007:     drop col_a;
0008:     drop col_b;
0009:     split train = 80%;
0010:     train dead_model = logistic_regression;
0011:     train live_model = decision_tree;
0012:     evaluate live_model;
0013:     train trailing_model = random_forest;
0014: }

FILE examples/complex_valid_drop_chain.pipe
0001: pipeline complex_valid_drop_chain {
0002:     load "students.csv";
0003:     select c1, c2, c3, c4, c5;
0004:     drop c1;
0005:     split train = 80%;
0006:     drop c2;
0007:     train model = decision_tree;
0008:     evaluate model;
0009: }

FILE examples/data/README.md
0001: # Data Directory
0002: 
0003: The DSL currently validates dataset filenames as string literals.
0004: The compiler does not read the contents of the referenced dataset.
0005: Therefore, no actual CSV file is required to run the demo.
0006: 
0007: Optionally, you could place a public dataset such as the UCI Student Performance dataset here for a realistic-looking future demo.

FILE examples/examples_demo.py
0001: import sys
0002: import os
0003: 
0004: # Ensure ds_pipeline_dsl is in PYTHONPATH
0005: sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', 'src')))
0006: 
0007: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import tokenize
0008: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser
0009: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import analyze, SemanticError
0010: 
0011: VALID_PROGRAM = '''
0012: pipeline student_prediction {
0013:     load "students.csv";
0014:     clean missing_values;
0015:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0016:     split train = 80%;
0017:     train model = decision_tree;
0018:     evaluate model;
0019: }
0020: '''
0021: 
0022: INVALID_PROGRAM = '''
0023: pipeline student_prediction {
0024:     load "students.csv";
0025:     clean missing_values;
0026:     select cgpa, attendance, study_hours;
0027:     train model = decision_tree;
0028:     evaluate model;
0029: }
0030: '''
0031: 
0032: def run_demo(source: str, label: str):
0033:     print(f"\n{'=' * 70}\n{label}\n{'=' * 70}")
0034:     tokens = tokenize(source)
0035:     print(f"\n[LEXER OUTPUT] {len(tokens) - 1} tokens generated:")
0036:     print(", ".join(f"{t.type}({t.value})" for t in tokens if t.type != "EOF"))
0037: 
0038:     ast = Parser(tokens).parse_pipeline()
0039:     print(f"\n[PARSER OUTPUT] AST for pipeline '{ast.name}':")
0040:     for s in ast.statements:
0041:         print("   ", s)
0042: 
0043:     print("\n[SEMANTIC ANALYSIS]")
0044:     try:
0045:         table = analyze(ast)
0046:         print("    No semantic errors found. Symbol table state:")
0047:         print(f"    data_loaded={table.data_loaded}, "
0048:               f"data_cleaned={table.data_cleaned}, "
0049:               f"splits={table.splits}, models={table.models}")
0050:     except SemanticError as e:
0051:         print(f"    {e}")
0052: 
0053: if __name__ == "__main__":
0054:     run_demo(VALID_PROGRAM, "DEMO 1: VALID PIPELINE (train after split)")
0055:     run_demo(INVALID_PROGRAM, "DEMO 2: INVALID PIPELINE (train before split)")
