0154: 
0155: @contextmanager
0156: def safe_render(label):
0157:     """Keep optional page/tab failures out of the main traceback view."""
0158:     try:
0159:         yield
0160:     except Exception:
0161:         st.warning(f"Could not display {label}. Please try again or choose another page.")
0162:         with st.expander("Technical details"):
0163:             st.code(traceback.format_exc(), language="text")
0164: 
0165: 
0166: def header(title, description):
0167:     st.markdown(f'<div class="hero"><div class="hero-brand"><img src="{LOGO_URL}" alt="">'
0168:                 '<div class="eyebrow">DATA SCIENCE PIPELINE DSL</div></div>'
0169:                 f'<h1>{escape(title)}</h1><p>{escape(description)}</p></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
0170: 
0171: 
0172: def digest(source):
0173:     return hashlib.sha256(source.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
0174: 
0175: 
0176: def source_view(source, error=None):
0177:     lines = source.split("\n")
0178:     # EOF errors may refer to a position one line beyond the supplied text.
0179:     if error and error.line == len(lines) + 1:
0180:         lines.append("")
0181:     rows = []
0182:     for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
0183:         line = line.removesuffix("\r")
0184:         highlight = error is not None and error.line == number
0185:         content = escape(line)
0186:         if highlight and error.kind == "Lexical" and error.col is not None:
0187:             col = error.col  # The compiler reports zero-based columns.
0188:             if 0 <= col < len(line):
0189:                 content = escape(line[:col]) + "<mark>" + escape(line[col]) + "</mark>" + escape(line[col + 1:])
0190:         css = ' class="error-line"' if highlight else ""
0191:         rows.append(f'<tr{css}><td class="line-no">{number}</td><td>{content or " "}</td></tr>')
0192:     st.markdown('<div class="source-wrap"><table class="source-table" aria-label="Compiled source">'
0193:                 + "".join(rows) + '</table></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
0194: 
0195: 
0196: def error_view(result):
0197:     error = result.error
0198:     location = f" - line {error.line}" if error.line is not None else ""
0199:     if error.col is not None:
0200:         location += f", col {error.col}"
0201:     if error.kind == "Internal":
0202:         st.error(f"Internal Error in {error.stage}. The compiler could not finish this source.")
0203:         with st.expander("Compiler traceback"):
0204:             st.code(result.traceback or error.message, language="text")
0205:     else:
0206:         st.error(f"{error.kind} Error{location}")
0207:         st.code(error.message, language="text")
0208:     if error.kind in {"Lexical", "Syntax", "Semantic"}:
0209:         detector = {"Lexical": "The lexer detects invalid characters and strings",
0210:                     "Syntax": "The parser detects grammar errors",
0211:                     "Semantic": "The semantic analyzer detects dependency and state errors"}[error.kind]
0212:         st.caption(f"{detector}; compilation stopped at {error.stage} after the first error.")
0213:     source_view(result.source, error)
0214: 
0215: 
0216: def show_table(rows):
0217:     st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows), hide_index=True, width="stretch")
0218: 
0219: 
0220: def ir_rows(instructions, labels=None):
0221:     rows = []
0222:     for i, instruction in enumerate(instructions, 1):
0223:         args = {k: v for k, v in instruction.args.items() if k != "line"}
0224:         row = {"index": i, "op": instruction.op, "args": repr(args), "line": instruction.args.get("line")}
0225:         if labels is not None:
0226:             row["status"] = labels[i - 1]
0227:         rows.append(row)
0228:     return rows
0229: 
0230: 
0231: def ir_table_html(instructions, labels=None):
0232:     """Presentation-only table: wrap real IR values instead of scrolling sideways."""
0233:     if not instructions:
0234:         return '<div class="ir-table-wrap"><div class="empty-ir">No IR instructions.</div></div>'
0235:     headings = ("No.", "Operation", "Arguments", "Line") + (("Change",) if labels is not None else ())
0236:     widths = ("7%", "21%", "48%", "9%", "15%") if labels is not None else ("7%", "23%", "60%", "10%")
0237:     rows = []
0238:     for row in ir_rows(instructions, labels):
0239:         cells = (f'<td>{row["index"]}</td><td class="ir-op">{escape(row["op"])}</td>'
0240:                  f'<td class="ir-args">{escape(row["args"])}</td>'
0241:                  f'<td>{escape(str(row["line"])) if row["line"] is not None else "—"}</td>')
0242:         if labels is not None:
0243:             status = row["status"]
0244:             css = "kept" if status == "kept" else "removed"
0245:             cells += f'<td><span class="ir-status {css}">{escape(status.replace("/", " / "))}</span></td>'
0246:         rows.append(f'<tr>{cells}</tr>')
0247:     return ('<div class="ir-table-wrap"><table class="ir-table" aria-label="IR instructions">'
0248:             '<colgroup>' + ''.join(f'<col style="width:{width}">' for width in widths) + '</colgroup>'
0249:             '<thead><tr>' + ''.join(f'<th scope="col">{escape(h)}</th>' for h in headings) + '</tr></thead>'
0250:             '<tbody>' + ''.join(rows) + '</tbody></table></div>')
0251: 
0252: 
0253: def reached(result, stage):
0254:     record = next(s for s in result.stages if s.name == stage)
0255:     if record.status == "skipped":
0256:         st.info(f"Not reached because compilation stopped at {result.failed_stage}")
0257:         return False
0258:     if record.status == "failed":
0259:         st.info(f"{stage} failed; no completed output is available.")
0260:         return False
0261:     return True
0262: 
0263: 
0264: def overview():
0265:     header("A pipeline, from source to symbolic trace.",
0266:            "Explore a compiler front-end for a Data Science pipeline language. Follow real source through lexical, "
0267:            "syntactic and semantic analysis, then inspect its intermediate representation, four optimizer passes and symbolic execution.")
0268:     st.markdown('<div class="language-label">Language: <strong>Data Science Pipeline DSL</strong> · .pipe</div>'
0269:                 '<div class="language-panel"><div class="declaration"><span class="section-label">Program declaration</span>'
0270:                 '<code>pipeline</code></div><div class="section-label">Pipeline operations</div><div class="chips">'
0271:                 + ''.join(f'<span class="chip">{escape(s)}</span>' for s in
0272:                           ("load", "clean", "select", "drop", "split", "train", "evaluate"))
0273:                 + '</div></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
0274:     examples = compiler_runner.list_examples(EXAMPLES_DIR)
0275:     facts = [("Example programs", str(len(examples)), "Discovered in the example library")]
0276:     evidence = ROOT / "docs" / "test_results.txt"
0277:     try:
0278:         match = re.search(r"(\d+) passed", evidence.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
0279:         if match:
0280:             facts.append(("Passed tests", match[1], "Saved report · not a live test run"))
0281:     except (OSError, UnicodeError):
0282:         pass
0283:     for column, (label, value, detail) in zip(st.columns(len(facts)), facts):
0284:         with column:
0285:             st.markdown(f'<div class="fact-card"><div class="section-label">{escape(label)}</div>'
0286:                         f'<div class="fact-value">{escape(value)}</div><div class="fact-detail">{escape(detail)}</div></div>',
0287:                         unsafe_allow_html=True)
0288:     st.subheader("The compilation journey")
0289:     st.markdown('<div class="flow">' + '<span class="flow-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</span>'.join(
0290:         f'<span class="flow-node">{escape(n)}</span>' for n in
0291:         ("Source", "Lexer", "Parser / AST", "Semantic / symbols", "IR", "Optimizer", "Symbolic executor", "Trace"))
0292:         + '</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
0293:     grammar = ROOT / "docs" / "grammar.md"
0294:     if grammar.is_file():
0295:         with st.expander("Language grammar"):
0296:             with st.container(key="grammar_reference"), safe_render("language grammar"):
0297:                 st.markdown(grammar.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
0298: 
0299: 
0300: def load_selected_example():
0301:     """Widget callback writes editor state before the text_area is instantiated."""
0302:     selected = st.session_state.get("example_choice")
0303:     if selected:
0304:         try:
0305:             st.session_state["editor_text"] = compiler_runner.read_example(EXAMPLES_DIR / selected)
0306:             st.session_state["example_notice"] = None
0307:         except (OSError, UnicodeError):
0308:             st.session_state["example_notice"] = f"Could not read {selected}. You can still edit and compile your source."
0309: 
0310: 
0311: def compilation_tabs(result):
0312:     names = ("Tokens", "AST", "Semantic Analysis", "IR", "Optimization", "Execution Trace")
0313:     for tab, name, stage in zip(st.tabs(names), names, compiler_runner.STAGE_NAMES):
0314:         with tab, safe_render(name):
0315:             if name == "Semantic Analysis" and result.error and result.error.kind == "Semantic":
0316:                 st.error(f"Semantic Error - line {result.error.line}")
0317:                 st.code(result.error.message, language="text")
0318:                 continue
0319:             if not reached(result, stage):
0320:                 continue
0321:             if name == "Tokens":
0322:                 show_table([dict(index=i, **asdict(t)) for i, t in enumerate(result.tokens[:1000], 1)])
0323:                 if len(result.tokens) > 1000:
0324:                     st.caption(f"showing first 1000 of {len(result.tokens)}")
0325:                     st.caption(f"Final token (outside displayed range): {result.tokens[-1]!r}")
0326:                 st.caption("Token columns use the compiler's zero-based coordinates. EOF is included in the token stream.")
0327:             elif name == "AST":
0328:                 st.write("Pipeline:", result.ast.name)
0329:                 rows = []
0330:                 for i, node in enumerate(result.ast.statements, 1):
0331:                     fields = asdict(node) if is_dataclass(node) else dict(vars(node))
0332:                     line = fields.pop("line", None)
0333:                     rows.append({"index": i, "node type": type(node).__name__, "fields": repr(fields), "line": line})
0334:                 show_table(rows)
0335:                 with st.expander("Raw AST"):
0336:                     st.json(asdict(result.ast) if is_dataclass(result.ast) else vars(result.ast))
0337:                     st.code(repr(result.ast), language="text")
0338:             elif name == "Semantic Analysis":
0339:                 st.success("No semantic errors")
0340:                 show_table([{"field": k, "value": repr(v)} for k, v in vars(result.symbol_table).items()])
0341:             elif name == "IR":
0342:                 st.markdown(ir_table_html(result.ir), unsafe_allow_html=True)
0343:             elif name == "Optimization":
0344:                 for col, label, value in zip(st.columns(4),
0345:                         ("IR before", "IR after", "Instructions removed", "Reduction"),
0346:                         (result.ir_before, result.ir_after, result.removed, f"{result.reduction_pct:.1f}%")):
0347:                     col.metric(label, value)
0348:                 original = ir_table_html(result.ir, compiler_runner.match_ir(result.ir, result.optimized_ir))
0349:                 optimized = ir_table_html(result.optimized_ir)
0350:                 st.markdown('<div class="ir-grid"><section class="ir-panel"><h4>Original IR</h4>' + original
0351:                             + '</section><section class="ir-panel"><h4>Optimized IR</h4>' + optimized
0352:                             + '</section></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
0353:                 st.caption("Best-effort alignment from real op/args equality; merged drops appear as removed/merged.")
0354:                 st.markdown("**Optimizer notes**")
0355:                 if result.notes:
0356:                     for note in result.notes:
0357:                         st.text(f"• {note}")
0358:                 else:
0359:                     st.info("no optimizations applied")

0361:                 if result.unoptimized_trace is not None:
0362:                     counts.append(f"Unoptimized: {len(result.unoptimized_trace)} trace lines")
0363:                 if result.stages[-1].status == "ok":
0364:                     counts.append(f"Optimized: {len(result.trace)} trace lines")

FILE dashboard/compiler_runner.py
0001: """Pure adapter over the existing compiler; no compiler behavior is replaced."""
0002: 
0003: from contextlib import redirect_stdout
0004: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
0005: from io import StringIO
0006: from pathlib import Path
0007: import re
0008: from time import perf_counter
0009: import traceback
0010: from typing import Literal
0011: 
0012: from ds_pipeline_dsl.lexer import LexicalError, Token, tokenize
0013: from ds_pipeline_dsl.parser import Parser, SyntaxError_
0014: from ds_pipeline_dsl.semantic import SemanticError, SymbolTable, analyze
0015: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ast_nodes import PipelineNode
0016: from ds_pipeline_dsl.ir import IRInstruction, lower_to_ir
0017: from ds_pipeline_dsl.optimizer import optimize
0018: from ds_pipeline_dsl.executor import execute
0019: 
0020: MAX_SOURCE_CHARS = 100_000
0021: STAGE_NAMES = ("Lexer", "Parser", "Semantic", "IR", "Optimizer", "Executor")
0022: 
0023: 
0024: @dataclass
0025: class StageResult:
0026:     name: str
0027:     status: Literal["ok", "failed", "skipped"] = "skipped"
0028:     ms: float = 0.0
0029:     count: int | None = None
0030: 
0031: 
0032: @dataclass
0033: class CompilerError:
0034:     kind: Literal["Lexical", "Syntax", "Semantic", "Internal", "Input"]
0035:     message: str
0036:     line: int | None
0037:     col: int | None
0038:     stage: str
0039: 
0040: 
0041: @dataclass
0042: class CompileResult:
0043:     source: str
0044:     stages: list[StageResult] = field(default_factory=lambda: [StageResult(n) for n in STAGE_NAMES])
0045:     tokens: list[Token] = field(default_factory=list)
0046:     ast: PipelineNode | None = None
0047:     symbol_table: SymbolTable | None = None
0048:     ir: list[IRInstruction] = field(default_factory=list)
0049:     optimized_ir: list[IRInstruction] = field(default_factory=list)
0050:     notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
0051:     trace: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
0052:     unoptimized_trace: list[str] | None = None
0053:     comparison_error: str | None = None
0054:     error: CompilerError | None = None
0055:     failed_stage: str | None = None
0056:     traceback: str | None = None
0057: 
0058:     @property
0059:     def ir_before(self) -> int:
0060:         return len(self.ir)
0061: 
0062:     @property
0063:     def ir_after(self) -> int:
0064:         return len(self.optimized_ir)
0065: 
0066:     @property
0067:     def removed(self) -> int:
0068:         return self.ir_before - self.ir_after
0069: 
0070:     @property
0071:     def reduction_pct(self) -> float:
0072:         return 100.0 * self.removed / self.ir_before if self.ir_before else 0.0
0073: 
0074: 
0075: def compile_source(source: str) -> CompileResult:
0076:     """Run each real stage, retaining completed stages after the first failure.
0077: 
0078:     Compiler columns are zero-based. Timing covers each primary stage only;
0079:     the optional unoptimized execution is a separate comparison.
0080:     """
0081:     result = CompileResult(source)
0082:     if len(source) > MAX_SOURCE_CHARS:
0083:         result.failed_stage = "Input"
0084:         result.error = CompilerError(
0085:             "Input", f"Source is too large. Please use at most {MAX_SOURCE_CHARS:,} characters.",
0086:             None, None, "Input",
0087:         )
0088:         return result
0089: 
0090:     actions = (
0091:         ("tokens", lambda: tokenize(source), len),
0092:         ("ast", lambda: Parser(result.tokens).parse_pipeline(), lambda a: len(a.statements)),
0093:         ("symbol_table", lambda: analyze(result.ast), lambda t: len(vars(t))),
0094:         ("ir", lambda: lower_to_ir(result.ast, result.symbol_table), len),
0095:         ("optimized_ir", lambda: optimize(result.ir, result.notes), len),
0096:         ("trace", lambda: execute(result.optimized_ir), len),
0097:     )
0098:     with redirect_stdout(StringIO()):
0099:         for record, (attribute, action, counter) in zip(result.stages, actions):
0100:             start = perf_counter()
0101:             try:
0102:                 value = action()
0103:                 setattr(result, attribute, value)
0104:                 record.count = counter(value)
0105:                 record.status = "ok"
0106:             except Exception as exc:
0107:                 record.status = "failed"
0108:                 result.failed_stage = record.name
0109:                 kind = next((k for cls, k in (
0110:                     (LexicalError, "Lexical"), (SyntaxError_, "Syntax"),
0111:                     (SemanticError, "Semantic"),
0112:                 ) if isinstance(exc, cls)), "Internal")
0113:                 message = str(exc)
0114:                 location = re.search(r"at line (\d+)(?:, col (\d+))?", message)
0115:                 result.error = CompilerError(
0116:                     kind, message, int(location[1]) if location else None,
0117:                     int(location[2]) if location and location[2] else None, record.name,
0118:                 )
0119:                 if kind == "Internal":
0120:                     result.traceback = traceback.format_exc()
0121:                 break
0122:             finally:
0123:                 record.ms = (perf_counter() - start) * 1000
0124: 
0125:         # A failed comparison must never replace the primary compilation result.
0126:         if result.stages[3].status == "ok":
0127:             try:
0128:                 result.unoptimized_trace = execute(result.ir)
0129:             except Exception:
0130:                 result.comparison_error = traceback.format_exc()
0131:     return result
0132: 
0133: 
0134: def match_ir(before: list[IRInstruction], after: list[IRInstruction]) -> list[str]:
0135:     """Best-effort two-pointer alignment derived from real (op, args) equality.
0136: 
0137:     A merged run is consumed at once and its original drops are all labelled
0138:     removed/merged. These labels are a display aid, not optimizer provenance.
0139:     """
0140:     labels = ["removed/merged"] * len(before)
0141:     i = j = 0
0142:     while i < len(before) and j < len(after):
0143:         original, optimized = before[i], after[j]
0144:         if (original.op, original.args) == (optimized.op, optimized.args):
0145:             labels[i] = "kept"
0146:             i += 1
0147:             j += 1
0148:         elif original.op == optimized.op == "OP_DROP":
0149:             end, columns = i, []
0150:             while end < len(before) and before[end].op == "OP_DROP":
0151:                 for col in before[end].args.get("columns", []):
0152:                     if col not in columns:
0153:                         columns.append(col)
0154:                 end += 1
0155:             merged = dict(original.args, columns=columns)
0156:             if end > i + 1 and merged == optimized.args:
0157:                 i, j = end, j + 1
0158:             else:
0159:                 i += 1
0160:         else:
0161:             i += 1
0162:     return labels
0163: 
0164: 
0165: @dataclass(frozen=True)
0166: class ExampleEntry:
0167:     path: Path
0168:     name: str
0169:     category: str
0170: 
0171: 
0172: def list_examples(examples_dir: str | Path) -> list[ExampleEntry]:
0173:     """Discover the repository's valid_, invalid_, complex_, optimizer_ prefixes."""
0174:     try:
0175:         entries = []
0176:         for path in Path(examples_dir).glob("*.pipe"):
0177:             if path.is_file():
0178:                 prefix = path.stem.partition("_")[0]
0179:                 category = prefix.title() if prefix in {"valid", "invalid", "complex", "optimizer"} else "Other"
0180:                 entries.append(ExampleEntry(path, path.name, category))
0181:         return sorted(entries, key=lambda e: (e.category, e.name))
0182:     except OSError:
0183:         return []
0184: 
0185: 
0186: def read_example(path: str | Path) -> str:
0187:     return Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")

FILE dashboard/performance_loader.py
0001: """Read recorded Markdown benchmark results without running benchmarks."""
0002: 
0003: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
0004: from pathlib import Path
0005: import re
0006: from typing import Any
0007: 
0008: 
0009: @dataclass
0010: class PerformanceData:
0011:     available: bool
0012:     reason: str = ""
0013:     table: Any = None
0014:     environment: str = ""
0015:     observations: str = ""
0016:     marked_rows: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)
0017: 
0018: 
0019: def _section(text: str, title: str) -> str:
0020:     """Return section body verbatim, retaining its original whitespace."""
0021:     match = re.search(rf"(?m)^([#]{{1,6}})[ \t]+{re.escape(title)}[ \t]*\r?\n", text)
0022:     if not match:
0023:         return ""
0024:     remaining = text[match.end():]
0025:     end = re.search(rf"(?m)^[#]{{1,{len(match[1])}}}[ \t]+", remaining)
0026:     return remaining[:end.start()] if end else remaining
0027: 
0028: 
0029: def load_performance(path: str | Path) -> PerformanceData:
0030:     try:
0031:         # newline='' keeps section bodies verbatim, including CRLF in reports.
0032:         with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
0033:             text = handle.read()
0034:     except (OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
0035:         return PerformanceData(False, f"Could not read benchmark report: {exc}")
0036:     try:
0037:         import pandas as pd
0038: 
0039:         lines = text.splitlines()
0040:         for i in range(len(lines) - 1):
0041:             if "|" not in lines[i]:
0042:                 continue
0043:             headers = [c.strip() for c in lines[i].strip().strip("|").split("|")]
0044:             separator = [c.strip() for c in lines[i + 1].strip().strip("|").split("|")]
0045:             if len(headers) < 2 or len(set(headers)) != len(headers) or not all(headers):
0046:                 continue
0047:             if len(separator) != len(headers) or not all(re.fullmatch(r":?-{3,}:?", c) for c in separator):
0048:                 continue
0049:             # Ignore unrelated prose tables; a benchmark table has Statements.
0050:             if "Statements" not in headers:
0051:                 continue
0052:             rows = []
0053:             for line in lines[i + 2:]:
0054:                 if "|" not in line or not line.strip():
0055:                     break
0056:                 cells = [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]
0057:                 if len(cells) != len(headers):
0058:                     return PerformanceData(False, "The benchmark table contains a malformed row.")
0059:                 rows.append(cells)
0060:             if not rows:
0061:                 continue
0062:             table = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=headers)
0063:             marked = []
0064:             if "Status" in table:
0065:                 marked = table.index[table["Status"].str.contains(r"skipp|reduc", case=False, regex=True)].tolist()
0066:             for column in table.columns:
0067:                 if column != "Status":
0068:                     table[column] = pd.to_numeric(table[column].str.removesuffix("%"), errors="coerce")
0069:             return PerformanceData(True, table=table, environment=_section(text, "Environment"),
0070:                                    observations=_section(text, "Observations"), marked_rows=marked)
0071:         return PerformanceData(False, "No readable benchmark results table was found.")
0072:     except Exception as exc:
0073:         return PerformanceData(False, f"Could not parse benchmark results: {exc}")

FILE README.md
0001: # A Domain-Specific Language and Compiler Front-End for Data Science Pipelines
0002: 
0003: A robust, modular compiler front-end and symbolic execution engine for a data-science-pipeline domain-specific language (`ds_pipeline_dsl`). The system validates, lowers, optimizes, and simulates data science workflow declarations with complete lexical, syntactic, semantic, and intermediate representation (IR) guarantees.
0004: 
0005: ---
0006: 
0007: ## 1. Overview & Key Features
0008: 
0009: `ds_pipeline_dsl` is designed for declaring end-to-end data science pipelines (loading, cleaning, column selection, column dropping, partitioning, model training, and evaluation). It replaces unstructured configuration scripts with a statically validated language.
0010: 
0011: - **Modular Compiler Architecture**: Decoupled multi-pass pipeline: Lexer $\to$ Recursive-Descent Parser $\to$ AST $\to$ Semantic Analyzer (SymbolTable) $\to$ IR Lowering $\to$ Four-Pass Optimizer $\to$ Symbolic Execution Engine.
0012: - **Strict Dependency & Semantic Validation**: Enforces dataset availability, partition constraints ($0 < p < 100$, cumulative $\le 100\%$), strict data-preparation ordering before training, column set disjointness, duplicate detection, and model life-cycle checks.
0013: - **Safe Linear-Time ($O(n)$) Optimization**: Four independent passes eliminate unreachable instructions, dead model training, duplicate consecutive cleans/selects, and merge contiguous drop statements.
0014: - **Symbolic Simulation Engine**: Fast, deterministic execution trace generation without invoking heavy machine learning libraries or reading external CSV data.
0015: - **Complete Test & Benchmark Suite**: 223 automated unit and integration tests passing in under 0.3s; reproducible performance benchmark demonstrating linear scaling across 10,000 statements in 0.67s.
0016: 
0017: ---
0018: 
0019: ## 2. Project Layout
0020: 
0021: ```text
0022: .
0023: ├── src/ds_pipeline_dsl/      # Core compiler package
0024: │   ├── __init__.py           # Package exports and version metadata
0025: │   ├── ast_nodes.py          # Typed AST dataclasses for pipeline nodes and statements
0026: │   ├── cli.py                # Command-line driver and output formatter
0027: │   ├── executor.py           # Symbolic execution engine generating trace logs
0028: │   ├── ir.py                 # Intermediate representation dataclass and AST lowering
0029: │   ├── lexer.py              # Regex-based scanner with line and column coordinate tracking
0030: │   ├── optimizer.py          # Four-pass linear-time dead code & redundancy optimizer
0031: │   ├── parser.py             # LL(1) recursive-descent parser producing validated AST
0032: │   └── semantic.py           # Symbol table state tracking and semantic validation rules
0033: ├── tests/                    # Comprehensive pytest test suite (223 tests)
0034: │   ├── helpers.py            # Test helpers: compile_to_ir, evaluation_records, assert_order_preserving
0035: │   ├── test_benchmark.py     # Benchmark structural integrity, counts, and safeguard tests
0036: │   ├── test_boundary.py      # Edge cases: 200 cols, 1k stmts, single-line, 5k ident, boundaries
0037: │   ├── test_cli.py           # CLI invocation, argument flags, exit codes, trace formatting
0038: │   ├── test_examples.py      # Parametrized validation table covering every .pipe file in examples/
0039: │   ├── test_executor.py      # Symbolic execution traces and opcode format validation
0040: │   ├── test_integration.py   # Multi-stage integration tests, clean error exits without tracebacks
0041: │   ├── test_ir.py            # AST-to-IR lowering and opcode mapping tests
0042: │   ├── test_lexer.py         # Token scanning, CRLF, non-ASCII handling, error diagnostics
0043: │   ├── test_optimizer.py     # Equivalence, idempotence, positive, negative, and sensitivity tests
0044: │   ├── test_parser.py        # Grammar constraints, missing delimiters, syntax error checks
0045: │   └── test_semantic.py      # Comprehensive semantic rules pass/fail unit tests
0046: ├── examples/                 # Canonical DSL programs (.pipe files)
0047: │   ├── valid_basic.pipe      # Baseline canonical 6-statement pipeline
0048: │   ├── valid_drop.pipe       # Pipeline showcasing column drop before selection
0049: │   ├── ...                   # 20 curated valid, invalid, and complex pipeline examples
0050: ├── benchmarks/               # Performance measurement
0051: │   └── benchmark.py          # Automated benchmark with safeguards, quadratic projection, and report generator
0052: ├── docs/                     # Technical documentation & captured evidence
0053: │   ├── architecture.md       # Architectural flowchart, ASCII diagram, and IR specification
0054: │   ├── grammar.md            # Formal EBNF grammar and lexical specification
0055: │   ├── performance.md        # Benchmark measurements, scaling factors, and observations
0056: │   ├── test_results.txt      # Full pytest execution log (223 passed)
0057: │   ├── demo.md               # 5-minute presentation script and viva quick reference
0058: │   ├── demo_outputs/         # Captured stdout/stderr logs for all demo commands
0059: │   ├── screenshots/          # Suggested screenshots guide for submission
0060: │   └── report_and_presentation_checklist.md # Mapping to university manual chapters & criteria
0061: ├── pyproject.toml            # Project packaging specification
0062: └── README.md                 # Project documentation and user manual
0063: ```
0064: 
0065: ---
0066: 
0067: ## 3. Installation & Usage
0068: 
0069: ### 3.1 Requirements & Installation
0070: - **Python**: Version 3.12 or newer.
0071: - **Dependencies**: Python standard library only (no external dependencies required). `pytest` is used for running tests.
0072: 
0073: Install the package in editable mode from the repository root:
0074: ```bash
0075: pip install -e .
0076: ```
0077: 
0078: ### 3.2 Running the Compiler via CLI
0079: The compiler driver is accessible via the module CLI:
0080: ```bash
0081: python -m ds_pipeline_dsl.cli <path_to_pipe_file> [flags]
0082: ```
0083: 
0084: #### Available Flags
0085: - `--tokens`: Display the scanned token stream with line and column numbers.
0086: - `--ast`: Display the parsed Abstract Syntax Tree representation.
0087: - `--ir`: Display the unoptimized linear Intermediate Representation instructions.
0088: - `--optimized-ir`: Display the optimized IR instructions and diagnostic optimization notes.
0089: - `--trace`: Display the simulated execution trace produced by the symbolic engine.
0090: - *(No flags specified)*: By default, displays all five stages sequentially.
0091: 
0092: #### Exit Codes
0093: - `0`: Successful compilation and execution.
0094: - `1`: Error encountered during lexical analysis, parsing, semantic analysis, or file reading. Errors print a clean, single-line message to `stderr`/`stdout` without Python stack traces.
0095: 
0096: ---
0097: 
0098: ## 4. Language Reference (All 8 Statements)
0099: 
0100: | Statement | Syntax | Purpose & Description | Example |
0101: |:---|:---|:---|:---|
0102: | `pipeline` | `pipeline <IDENT> { <statements> }` | Top-level pipeline block container. Exactly one per file. | `pipeline student_model { ... }` |
0103: | `load` | `load "<filepath>";` | Loads the source dataset. Exactly one load required per pipeline. | `load "students.csv";` |
0104: | `clean` | `clean <strategy>;` | Applies a data cleaning strategy (free identifier). | `clean missing_values;` |
0105: | `select` | `select <col1>, <col2>, ...;` | Selects feature columns. Replaces prior column selection. | `select cgpa, attendance, hours;` |
0106: | `drop` | `drop <col1>, <col2>, ...;` | Drops specified columns. Cannot drop columns not in current selection. | `drop student_id, name;` |
0107: | `split` | `split <name> = <percent>%;` | Partitions dataset ($0 < p < 100$; cumulative total $\le 100\%$). | `split train = 80%;` |
0108: | `train` | `train <model> = <algorithm>;` | Trains model using algorithm (free identifier). Requires `train` split. | `train model1 = decision_tree;` |
0109: | `evaluate` | `evaluate <model>;` | Evaluates a previously trained model. Can be called multiple times. | `evaluate model1;` |
0110: 
0111: ---
0112: 
0113: ## 5. Diagnostic Errors & Error Classes
0114: 
0115: The compiler halts on the first encountered error and produces clean, line-referenced diagnostic messages:
0116: 
0117: ### 5.1 Lexical Errors (`LexicalError`)
0118: - **Unterminated string literal**: `Lexical Error at line 2, col 9: unterminated string literal`
0119: - **Unexpected character**: `Lexical Error at line 3, col 12: unexpected character '@'`
0120: 
0121: ### 5.2 Syntax Errors (`SyntaxError_`)
0122: - **Expected token mismatch**: `Syntax Error at line 2: expected SEMI, found IDENT ('clean')`
0123: - **Missing closing brace / unexpected EOF**: `Syntax Error at line 5: unexpected end of file; expected '}' to close pipeline 'p'`
0124: - **Multiple pipelines**: `Syntax Error at line 8: only one pipeline per file is supported`
0125: - **Unexpected token after pipeline**: `Syntax Error at line 10: unexpected token IDENT ('extra') after end of pipeline`
0126: - **Invalid percentage format**: `Syntax Error at line 4: expected PERCENT, found NUMBER ('80')`
0127: - **Missing column in select/drop**: `Syntax Error at line 3: expected IDENT, found SEMI (';')`
0128: 
0129: ### 5.3 Semantic Errors (`SemanticError`)
0130: All 21 semantic error conditions checked in [`semantic.py`](src/ds_pipeline_dsl/semantic.py):
0131: 1. **Duplicate load**: `Semantic Error at line 3: 'load' used more than once; a dataset was already loaded at line 2.`
0132: 2. **Empty load filename**: `Semantic Error at line 2: 'load' requires a non-empty filename.`
0133: 3. **Clean before load**: `Semantic Error at line 2: 'clean' used before any 'load' statement. No dataset is available.`
0134: 4. **Clean after train**: `Semantic Error at line 5: 'clean' used after model 'm' was trained at line 4; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'.`
0135: 5. **Select before load**: `Semantic Error at line 2: 'select' used before any 'load' statement.`
0136: 6. **Select after train**: `Semantic Error at line 5: 'select' used after model 'm' was trained at line 4; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'.`
0137: 7. **Duplicate column in select**: `Semantic Error at line 3: column 'cgpa' appears more than once in 'select'.`
0138: 8. **Select of dropped column**: `Semantic Error at line 4: column 'c1' was dropped at line 3 and cannot be selected.`
0139: 9. **Drop before load**: `Semantic Error at line 2: 'drop' used before any 'load' statement.`
0140: 10. **Drop after train**: `Semantic Error at line 5: 'drop' used after model 'm' was trained at line 4; data-preparation statements must appear before any 'train'.`
0141: 11. **Duplicate column in drop**: `Semantic Error at line 3: column 'c1' appears more than once in 'drop'.`
0142: 12. **Drop already dropped column**: `Semantic Error at line 4: column 'c1' was already dropped at line 3.`
0143: 13. **Drop column not in selection**: `Semantic Error at line 4: cannot drop column 'c3': it is not in the current selection.`
0144: 14. **Drop all selected columns**: `Semantic Error at line 4: 'drop' would remove every selected column.`
0145: 15. **Split before load**: `Semantic Error at line 2: 'split' used before any 'load' statement.`
0146: 16. **Split percentage out of bounds**: `Semantic Error at line 3: split percentage must be strictly between 0 and 100.`
0147: 17. **Duplicate split name**: `Semantic Error at line 4: split 'train' is already defined at line 3.`
0148: 18. **Cumulative split exceeds 100%**: `Semantic Error at line 4: cumulative split total would be 100.1%, which exceeds 100%.`
0149: 19. **Train before split definition**: `Semantic Error at line 3: training data has not been defined. A 'split train = <percent>%;' statement must appear before 'train m = dt;'.`
0150: 20. **Duplicate model name**: `Semantic Error at line 5: model 'm' is already defined at line 4.`
0151: 21. **Evaluate before train**: `Semantic Error at line 4: model 'm' is evaluated before it has been trained.`
0152: 
0153: ---
0154: 
0155: ## 6. Optimizer Architecture & Safety Principles
0156: 
0157: The optimizer [`optimizer.py`](src/ds_pipeline_dsl/optimizer.py) executes four independent, linear-time ($O(n)$) passes in strict program order:
0158: 
0159: 1. `_remove_unreachable`: Removes instructions appearing after the **last** `OP_EVAL`. If no evaluations exist, the IR is untouched.
0160: 2. `_remove_dead_trains`: Backward scan identifying models evaluated in the pipeline; eliminates `OP_TRAIN` instructions for models that are never evaluated.
0161: 3. `_merge_adjacent_drops`: Combines consecutive runs of $\ge 2$ `OP_DROP` instructions into a single merged drop with ordered, deduplicated columns.
0162: 4. `_collapse_duplicates`: Collapses consecutive identical `OP_CLEAN` or `OP_SELECT` instructions (ignoring source line numbers during comparison).
0163: 
0164: ### Safety Argument
0165: - **Observable Behavior**: The observable behavior of a pipeline is defined as the sequence of model evaluation records:
0166:   $$\text{Record} = (\text{model\_name}, \text{algorithm}, \text{train\_state}, \text{eval\_state})$$
0167: - Instructions appearing after the last evaluation produce no observable records and affect no subsequent evaluations.
0168: - Models trained but never evaluated write only to internal model state that is never read. Removing them produces zero observable difference.
0169: - Because data-preparation statements cannot appear after model training (enforced by semantic rules), dead train elimination never makes previously separated data-preparation statements adjacent.
0170: - If a pipeline contains no `evaluate` statement, the optimizer conservatively preserves all instructions without pruning.
0171: 
0172: ---
0173:

0183: | [`optimizer_demo.pipe`](examples/optimizer_demo.pipe) | Exercises all 4 optimizer passes in one program | Compiles, optimizes (11 $\to$ 7), executes (Exit 0) |
0184: | [`complex_multi_model.pipe`](examples/complex_multi_model.pipe) | Multi-model evaluation chain with dead train removal | Compiles & executes both evaluations (Exit 0) |
0185: | [`complex_redundant.pipe`](examples/complex_redundant.pipe) | Heavy redundancies (cleans, selects, drops, dead trains) | Compiles, optimizes (12 $\to$ 7), executes (Exit 0) |
0186: | [`complex_valid_drop_chain.pipe`](examples/complex_valid_drop_chain.pipe) | Non-adjacent drops separated by a split statement | Compiles, preserves separate drops, executes (Exit 0) |
0187: | [`invalid_clean_after_train.pipe`](examples/invalid_clean_after_train.pipe) | Data cleaning after model training | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0188: | [`invalid_drop_after_train.pipe`](examples/invalid_drop_after_train.pipe) | Dropping columns after model training | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0189: | [`invalid_drop_all_selected.pipe`](examples/invalid_drop_all_selected.pipe) | Dropping all currently selected columns | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0190: | [`invalid_drop_before_load.pipe`](examples/invalid_drop_before_load.pipe) | Dropping columns before dataset loading | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0191: | [`invalid_drop_twice.pipe`](examples/invalid_drop_twice.pipe) | Dropping the same column multiple times | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0192: | [`invalid_duplicate_load.pipe`](examples/invalid_duplicate_load.pipe) | Loading multiple datasets in one pipeline | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0193: | [`invalid_duplicate_model.pipe`](examples/invalid_duplicate_model.pipe) | Training multiple models with identical names | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0194: | [`invalid_duplicate_split.pipe`](examples/invalid_duplicate_split.pipe) | Defining multiple splits with identical names | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0195: | [`invalid_lexical_error.pipe`](examples/invalid_lexical_error.pipe) | Illegal character (`@`) in pipeline body | Lexical Error (Exit 1) |
0196: | [`invalid_select_dropped_column.pipe`](examples/invalid_select_dropped_column.pipe) | Selecting a column that was previously dropped | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0197: | [`invalid_split_total_over_100.pipe`](examples/invalid_split_total_over_100.pipe) | Cumulative split percentages exceeding 100% | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |
0198: | [`invalid_syntax_error.pipe`](examples/invalid_syntax_error.pipe) | Syntax error (missing statement semicolon) | Syntax Error (Exit 1) |
0199: | [`invalid_train_before_split.pipe`](examples/invalid_train_before_split.pipe) | Training a model before defining the `train` split | Semantic Error (Exit 1) |

0220: 
0221: ## 9. Scope & Limitations
0222: 
0223: 1. **Symbolic Execution Only**: The compiler analyzes and simulates pipeline execution logic; it does not read physical CSV files or execute numeric machine learning routines.
0224: 2. **Single Pipeline per File**: Exactly one pipeline declaration per `.pipe` file.
0225: 3. **Fail-Fast Error Handling**: Emits diagnostic messages on the first encountered lexical, syntactic, or semantic error.
0226: 4. **Reserved Keywords**: Language keywords (`load`, `clean`, `select`, `drop`, `split`, `train`, `evaluate`, `pipeline`) cannot be used as column or model names.
0227: 5. **Free Identifiers**: Strategy names in `clean` and algorithm names in `train` are free identifiers to accommodate arbitrary data science frameworks.
0228: 
0229: 
0230: ## Web Dashboard
0231: 
0232: The Streamlit dashboard presents the existing compiler for a live university demo: real tokens, AST, semantic state, original and optimized IR, optimizer notes, and symbolic execution traces. The compiler is unchanged. Execution is symbolic only: no CSV is read, no model is trained, and no numeric accuracy or prediction is produced. The UI runs locally and works offline after dependencies are installed; it makes no external network calls.
0233: 
0234: From the repository root in PowerShell, run these five commands:
0235: 
0236: ```powershell
0237: python -m venv .venv
0238: .venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1
0239: python -m pip install -e .
0240: python -m pip install -r requirements-dashboard.txt
0241: python -m streamlit run dashboard/app.py
0242: ```
0243: 
0244: Open http://localhost:8501. If PowerShell blocks activation, first run `Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope Process -ExecutionPolicy Bypass`, then activate again. If `streamlit` is not on PATH, always use `python -m streamlit` as above. If port 8501 is busy, launch with `python -m streamlit run dashboard/app.py --server.port 8502` and open http://localhost:8502. Stop the server with Ctrl+C.
0245: 
0246: The dashboard inserts the local `src/` directory into its import path, so it also works without an editable package install. The dashboard dependency file adds only Streamlit (tested with 1.66.0); pandas is supplied by Streamlit. No system Graphviz installation is needed.
0247: 
0248: Page guide:
0249: 
0250: - **Overview**: project purpose, language statements, discovered example count, saved test evidence if present, and the language grammar if available.
0251: - **Compiler Playground**: choose an example category, edit the source, press Compile, and inspect the six output tabs. Results remain visible when the editor changes, with a notice that they describe the previous source. Errors retain the compiler's original message and highlight its source location; columns use the compiler's zero-based numbering.
0252: - **Architecture**: the real compilation flow, semantic checks, four optimizer passes in execution order, and stage colors from the last compile.
0253: - **Performance**: the saved benchmark table, environment, stage-time and IR charts, and verbatim observations. It reads `docs/performance.md`; `python benchmarks/benchmark.py` creates that report. The dashboard never runs a benchmark automatically, and missing reports produce a friendly message.
0254: 
0255: Five-step live demo (about five minutes):
0256: 
0257: 1. Open Compiler Playground, compile `valid_basic.pipe`, and inspect Tokens, AST, Semantic Analysis, IR and Execution Trace.
0258: 2. Compile `invalid_lexical_error.pipe`, `invalid_syntax_error.pipe` and `invalid_train_before_split.pipe`; explain the error class, highlighted line and skipped later stages.
0259: 3. Compile `optimizer_demo.pipe`, open Optimization, and compare the real instruction counts, removed/merged labels, optimizer notes and trace-line counts.
0260: 4. Open Architecture to show the successful stage colors and the four ordered optimizer passes. Return after an invalid compilation to demonstrate failed/skipped colors.
0261: 5. Open Performance to discuss the recorded environment, scaling and IR reduction without launching a new benchmark during the presentation.
0262: 
0263: Run all compiler and dashboard tests from the repository root:
0264: 
0265: ```powershell
0266: python -m pytest -q
0267: ```
0268: 
0269: Pure runner tests always run; UI smoke tests use `streamlit.testing.v1.AppTest` and skip cleanly when Streamlit is absent. Benchmark parsing tests requiring pandas skip when pandas is absent. The dashboard handles empty sources using the real compiler, preserves partial results after a failure, and rejects sources longer than 100,000 characters. Token tables display at most 1,000 rows and retain the final EOF token in a caption for longer streams. IR kept/removed/merged labels are a best-effort comparison of real instruction data, not optimizer provenance. Trace counts can differ because optimization changes the symbolic instruction log; they are not a claim of identical full traces.
